Nothing in `packages/schema`, `packages/sim-ref`, `packages/sim-gpu`, `docs/rules.md` or the preregistration.

## 9. Tests (named cases)

- `movinglight-schedule`: `meridian equals RefSim.light argmax for fixed, paused, wandering` ; `realised integer path frozen, first-move, turn step and realised speed ratio reported` ; `reversed schedule has eastward and westward legs of at least k steps` ; `scored-post window starts at the realised turn` ; `no new WorldConfig key and defaultConfig hash unchanged`.
- `movinglight-observe`: `signed circular distance is exact at the wrap` ; `absolute error is nonzero for bodies at -8 and +8` ; `cohesion is continuous across the circular seam` ; `temporal lag is causal and unavailable when never coincident` ; `capture uses scored-window lightIn increments` ; `instrumented twin has the same stateHash sequence as plain` ; `conservation residual zero at every census`.
- `movinglight-arms`: `genome edits touch only the listed weights and hash stably` ; `sense-nosig emits nothing and still moves, sense-nomot emits and does not move` ; `patches are copied by translation with identical lineage ids and audited B/E` ; `dispersed cross-talk is measured and flagged above threshold` ; `grouped and dispersed share offsets from the t=0 meridian` ; `replicate-noise runs clone patches` ; `equal audited input energy across placements`.
- `movinglight-coordination`: `common forcing raises naive alignment in group and dispersed set equally and gives covariance ~0` ; `coupled agents give a residual above the floor` ; `static sun gives unavailable not zero` ; `zero-displacement and missing-lineage pairs are handled and pair counts reported`.
- `movinglight-fixtures`: `common-forcing is rejected while naive metric passes` ; `blind-drifters-physical is rejected` ; `crowding-physical is rejected` ; `stationary-growth and label-takeover are rejected` ; `coupled-synthetic is coordination-detected` ; `planted-benefit gives supported through the full decision` ; `null-world is exactly zero`.
- `movinglight-decision`: `needs raw S, B, sign test, delta floor, bootstrap bound, residual, survival and B_drift all together` ; `positive B with negative S is not supported` ; `7 of 8 gives p = 9/256` ; `zeros count as not positive` ; `extinct arms keep terminal outcomes and blocks are not dropped` ; `paused control with a benefit gives inconclusive-confounded` ; `floors come from noise seeds only`.
- `movinglight-evidence`: `canonical JSON refuses floats` ; `evidence payload hash equals manifest` ; `lock before write is required` ; `replay regenerates byte-equal evidence` (small panel).
- `tools/test/movinglight-evidence.test.ts`: `--check passes on saved artifacts` ; `--check fails on a tampered evidence byte` ; `--replay-check on the saved panel`.
- `apps/lab/test/moving-light-render.test.ts`: `blinded labels hide names until reveal` ; `naive and residual both rendered with the fixture rejection` ; `schedule switch changes the strip data` ; `escapes evidence strings`. `moving-light-packaging.test.ts`: as the niche test (vite entry, Dockerfile rm, Caddy 404 regex, caption, noindex, self-contained asset graph).

## 10. Acceptance gates (runnable)

```
export PATH=$HOME/.nvm/versions/node/v24.2.0/bin:$PATH
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm typecheck
pnpm vitest run --maxWorkers=2 packages/runner/test/movinglight-*.test.ts tools/test/movinglight-evidence.test.ts apps/lab/test/moving-light-*.test.ts
pnpm vitest run --maxWorkers=2 packages/sim-ref/test/golden-hashes.test.ts packages/schema/test    # pins and defaultConfig untouched
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --bench          # informational runtime projection
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --calibrate      # dev seeds only
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --freeze         # then commit and push the freeze files BEFORE --write
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --write
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --check
node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --replay-check
pnpm test -- --maxWorkers=2                            # full suite before the final commit
```
G1 the bench projection is a pre-freeze stop gate: full `--write` projected under about 10 minutes with at most two worker processes (`--maxWorkers=2` bounds Vitest only, so the tool enforces its own two-process cap); `--replay-check` on a frozen 2-block subset under about 2 minutes; over-target stops the stage and is reported, limits are not loosened. G2 `git diff` shows no change under `packages/schema`, `packages/sim-ref`, `packages/sim-gpu`. G3 every fixture and the saved evidence verify. G4 the freeze commit contains only `protocol.lock.json`, `PLAN.md`, `REVIEW.md` (plan review), the protocol/source files and seed registry; no outcome file; pushed to `origin/claude/i08-moving-light` before `--write`. G5 Codex (gpt-6.1-sol, xhigh, read-only) reviews the plan before freeze and the source plus evidence before commit; P1/P2 resolved or recorded in REVIEW.md.

## 11. Non-goals and limits

- No new physics, no Boids-like alignment actuator (a later rule fork only if this rung shows a gap an actuator would close), no new `WorldConfig` key, no GPU evidence (CPU only, stated), no evolution (mutation off), no paid calls, no deploy, no edit of the frozen preregistration.
- Small worlds, short horizons: results do not transfer to the 256 x 256 planet presets or to open-ended evolution.
- Reversal is a wander turning point (G1); a live reverse-the-sun control on physics is not available (G2); a public world is out of scope, only a local replay page.
- Capture is the realised `lightIn` increment per audited input energy; exposure and biomass change are covariates. Exposure differences across arms are reported, not corrected away.
- Signal cost and information are not separated: `sense` vs `sense-nosig` is a total strategy effect. Composition transfer is deferred. Solo baselines are descriptive only.
- Calibration may find the assay insensitive; then the label is `unsupported-measurement`, not "no benefit".
- One PR of reviewable size: about 9 source files of a few hundred lines each, the tool, the page, seven test files, and generated evidence. If the cost gate forces cuts, the page and transfer schedules shrink before the screen does.

| 3 | P1 | costed-null does not separate cost from information | Accepted. `sense-costed-null` dropped; `sense-nosig` (movement-capable) is the comparator; effect labelled total strategy effect, no information-only claim (4.2, 11). |

Confirmed by the review (no change): named genomes and public `RefSim.light` exist; zeroing EMIT/motility is valid; nonnegative `dayPeriod` and static meridian at dayPeriod 0; seed range free; 7 of 8 positives gives p = 9/256; Vite/Docker/Caddy niche precedent as described; G2 is accurate; no physics/hash change needed.

## Implementation-stage deviations from PLAN v2 (recorded before the freeze commit; dev and replicate-noise data only)

1. **Sensitivity bound `maxFloorCapPpm` = 500,000 (added; PLAN v2 only had "sense tracks on dev").** Calibration measured the replicate-noise floors: delta_cap = 1,429,742 ppm (fixed), 398,139 (paused), 2,592,988 (wandering), 2,222,461 (reversed, after the exact-turn window fix) of the audited inoculum input energy. The fixed floor is 143 percent of the audited input energy, which makes criterion (ii) practically unclearable (capture per input energy is not bounded by one, so this is an operational sensitivity choice, not a mathematical impossibility). The added rule says: if the fixed-schedule capture floor exceeds half the audited input energy (the dev grouped `sense` capture medians were about 400,000 to 640,000 ppm, so the bound encodes "the floor may not exceed the typical scale of the readout it bounds"), the assay is insensitive and the label is `unsupported-measurement`, not "no benefit". The bound was chosen after seeing dev floors (honest: it is a post-hoc sensitivity rule, but it uses dev and noise seeds only, is frozen in `protocol.lock.json` before any fresh seed is read, and the full contrast family, controls and decision are still run and reported). Consequence: the screen is `unsupported-measurement` before any fresh block is read; the fresh seeds 8700501-8700508 are consumed by this run, and a sharper geometry (more bodies, a larger tile, longer horizon, or a lower-variance capture readout) needs new fresh seeds.
2. **`sense-nosig` is dynamically identical to `still`** (identical capture, survival, tracking and coordination numbers in all 40 dev and noise runs compared; the state hashes differ only because the genome words differ): without signal the darkFlee movement offsets cancel. N equals the `still` contrast, `crowding-physical` is zero by identity, and the plan's "movement-capable information-free comparator" does not separate "could move" from "does not move". Recorded in the sealed LIMITS.
3. **Cross-talk** (dev seed 1, fixed): dispersed 958,904 ppm, grouped 963,768 ppm of the neighbourhood signal is foreign; the dispersed geometry is flagged and the claim stays "dispersed". The 24 x 16 tile is effectively one signal field.
4. **Patches vary per block** (patch seed = block seed, so the 8 fresh blocks differ in biomass noise) and replicate-noise runs clone the dev seed 1 patches; floors are the largest spread of each contrast and residual over the four noise seeds, per schedule.
5. **Geometry and schedule values fixed on dev seeds** (PLAN 4.1/4.2 left them to calibration): tile 24 x 16, four radius-2 bodies of mean biomass 96 per cell, nutrient 64, grouped as a 2 x 2 block six cells apart, dispersed at 12 x 8 spacing, both centred four cells west of the t=0 meridian (equal mean offset); T = 1,024; fixed P = 1,024 (about 1.5 times the blind drifter's speed), wander WP = 1,024 and A = 12 cells; census 32; warm-up 128. Cohesion is computed on the four lineage centroids (the biomass field fills the tile, so a mass-based cohesion is almost always unavailable).
6. **Cross-lineage displacement jumps** above six cells per census interval are excluded from the coordination statistic as label hand-overs (a frozen constant, used by the `label-takeover` fixture).
7. Page strips store the first fresh block only; `replay-check` regenerates the first two fresh blocks and the cheap synthetic fixtures (physical fixtures are checked by the saved-artifact path only).

| P2 | Completeness measured against the supplied array | Accepted. The frozen eight is the denominator in `decideSchedule`; seven passing blocks cannot be supported (test added). |

| P3 | "No finite contrast could clear" overclaims | Accepted. Reworded as an operational sensitivity choice. |
| P3 | Synthetic RNG multiplication beyond 2^53 | Accepted. `Math.imul` mixing. |

Confirmed by the review (no change): no frozen quantity reads fresh data; floors recompute from dev and replicate-noise seeds; the 500,000 cutoff is eligible for prospective freezing and `unsupported-measurement` precedes benefit labels; contrast signs, doubled-median comparisons, `signTail(7,8) = 9/256`, bootstrap indexing, capture increments, circular distances, centroid unwrapping, lag, cohesion and the 2^53 bounds are consistent.

Further own fix after the review: `comparatorsDisagree` now means one comparator's median B (or B_drift) is above twice the floor while the other's median is at or below zero (sign flips of noise no longer read as disagreement). The reversed floors were recalibrated after the exact-turn census change (delta_cap 2,222,461, delta_res 178,722,175); fixed, paused and wandering floors were reproduced unchanged.

    35	  fresh: { seeds: number[]; blocks: BlockView[]; decisions: Record<string, DecisionView>; verdict: { label: string; reasons: string[]; schedules: Record<string, { core: boolean; allCriteria: boolean }> } };
    36	  fixtures: { coordination: CoordFixtureView[]; physical: PhysFixtureView[]; planted: { id: string; verdict: { label: string } }[] };
    37	  calibration: { sensitivity: { senseTracks: boolean }; floors: Record<string, { cap: number; res: number }>; crossTalk: { dispersed: { foreignPpm: number }; flagged: boolean }; schedules: { schedule: string; firstMoveStep: number | null; turnStep: number | null; speedRatioPermille: number }[] };
    38	  limits: string[];
    39	  gaps: string[];
    40	}
    41	
    42	export const VERDICT_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
    43	  supported: 'A group-level interaction benefit is supported in this panel, world size, horizon and schedule set.',
    44	  'unsupported-within-tested-domain': 'No interaction benefit detected here. This says nothing about other worlds, sizes or sensors.',
    45	  'inconclusive-confounded': 'Inconclusive: a control or a comparator disagrees, so the benefit cannot be pinned on the interaction.',
    46	  inconclusive: 'Inconclusive: the estimates lean positive but the pre-stated bar was not met.',
    47	  'invalid-or-incomplete': 'The run was invalid or incomplete. Not a negative result.',
    48	  'unsupported-measurement': 'The assay was too noisy to answer the question (or the sensor did not track the sun at the dev stage): the engine-seed noise of the capture readout is larger than the readout itself. Not a negative result.',
    49	};
    50	
    51	export function verdictHtml(ev: EvidenceView): string {
    52	  const v = ev.fresh.verdict;
    53	  const rows = Object.entries(v.schedules).map(([id, s]) => `<li><strong>${esc(SCHEDULE_TEXT[id]?.name ?? id)}</strong>: persistence checks ${s.core ? 'passed' : 'not passed'}${id === 'fixed' ? `; all screening checks ${s.allCriteria ? 'passed' : 'not passed'}` : ''}</li>`).join('');
    54	  return `<p class="verdict-text" data-label="${esc(v.label)}"><strong>${esc(v.label)}</strong>. ${esc(VERDICT_TEXT[v.label] ?? '')}</p><p>${v.reasons.map(esc).join(' ')}</p><ul>${rows}</ul>`;
    55	}
    56	
    57	/** Median of a list as a rounded integer (lower middle for even counts), or null. */
    58	export const medianOf = (xs: readonly number[]): number | null => {
    59	  if (!xs.length) return null;
    60	  const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    61	  return s[(s.length - 1) >> 1];
    62	};
    63	
    64	export interface PanelStat { capturePpm: number | null; absErr1000: number | null; survivalPpm: number | null; extinct: number; blocks: number }
    65	export function panelStat(blocks: BlockView[], schedule: string, strategy: string, placement: 'grouped' | 'dispersed'): PanelStat {
    66	  const runs = blocks.map((b) => b.runs[schedule]?.[`${strategy}/${placement}`]).filter((r): r is RunView => !!r);
    67	  const errs = runs.map((r) => r.readouts.absErr1000).filter((x): x is number => x !== null);
    68	  return { capturePpm: medianOf(runs.map((r) => r.readouts.capturePpm)), absErr1000: medianOf(errs), survivalPpm: medianOf(runs.map((r) => r.readouts.survivalPpm)), extinct: runs.filter((r) => r.readouts.extinct).length, blocks: runs.length };
    69	}
    70	
    71	export function panelHtml(blocks: BlockView[], schedule: string, strategy: Strategy, revealed: boolean): string {
    72	  const g = panelStat(blocks, schedule, strategy, 'grouped'), d = panelStat(blocks, schedule, strategy, 'dispersed');
    73	  const row = (name: string, s: PanelStat) => `<tr><th scope="row">${name}</th><td>${pct(s.capturePpm)}</td><td>${cells(s.absErr1000)}</td><td>${pct(s.survivalPpm)}</td><td>${s.extinct} of ${s.blocks}</td></tr>`;
    74	  const what = revealed ? `<p class="arm-what">${esc(NAMES[strategy].what)}</p>` : `<p class="arm-what">Name and wiring hidden until you reveal them.</p>`;
    75	  return `<li class="arm" data-panel="${BLIND[strategy]}"><div class="arm-head"><strong>${esc(labelOf(strategy, revealed))}</strong></div>${what}<svg class="strip" id="strip-${BLIND[strategy]}" viewBox="0 0 320 120" role="img" aria-label="${esc(labelOf(strategy, revealed))}: distance of the bodies from the sun over time, grouped and dispersed"></svg><div class="scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Strategy numbers, scrolls sideways"><table><caption>Median over ${g.blocks} fresh blocks</caption><thead><tr><th scope="col">Placement</th><th scope="col">Light captured per input energy</th><th scope="col">Distance from sun (cells)</th><th scope="col">Biomass kept</th><th scope="col">Died out</th></tr></thead><tbody>${row('Grouped', g)}${row('Dispersed', d)}</tbody></table></div></li>`;
    76	}
    77	
    78	/** SVG polyline points for a strip series: x over the run, y = signed distance to the sun in cells (clamped to +-12). */
    79	export function stripPoints(t: number[], d1000: number[], w = 320, h = 120): string {
    80	  if (!t.length) return '';
    81	  const t1 = t[t.length - 1] || 1;
    82	  return t.map((tt, i) => `${((tt / t1) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h / 2 - (Math.max(-12000, Math.min(12000, d1000[i])) / 12000) * (h / 2 - 4)).toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
    83	}
    84	export function stripSvg(ev: EvidenceView, schedule: string, strategy: Strategy, cursor: number): string {
    85	  const strip = ev.fresh.blocks[0]?.strip;
    86	  if (!strip) return '';
    87	  const lines = (['grouped', 'dispersed'] as const).map((pl, k) => {
    88	    const s = strip[`${schedule}/${strategy}/${pl}`];
    89	    return s ? `<polyline fill="none" stroke="${k === 0 ? '#9fe4c4' : '#f2c978'}" stroke-width="1.6" points="${stripPoints(s.t, s.d1000)}"/>` : '';
    90	  }).join('');
    91	  const s0 = strip[`${schedule}/${strategy}/grouped`];
    92	  const x = s0 ? ((s0.t[Math.min(cursor, s0.t.length - 1)] / (s0.t[s0.t.length - 1] || 1)) * 320).toFixed(1) : '0';
    93	  return `<line x1="0" x2="320" y1="60" y2="60" stroke="#f2dfb7" stroke-dasharray="3 3" stroke-width="1"/>${lines}<line x1="${x}" x2="${x}" y1="0" y2="120" stroke="#8fa3a0" stroke-width="1"/>`;
    94	}
    95	
    96	export function coordFixturesHtml(ev: EvidenceView): string {
    97	  return ev.fixtures.coordination.map((f) => {
    98	    const avail = f.residual.filter((x): x is number => x !== null);
    99	    const med = medianOf(avail);
   100	    const naive = medianOf(f.naiveGroupedPpm.filter((x): x is number => x !== null));
   101	    return `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)} (${esc(f.schedule)})</th><td>${f.naiveReads ? 'collective motion' : 'no pattern'}${naive === null ? '' : ` (${pct(naive)} of pairs move the same way)`}</td><td>${med === null ? 'unavailable' : fmt(med)} (floor ${fmt(f.floor)})</td><td>${esc(f.label)}</td><td>${f.rejected ? 'rejected' : 'detected'}</td></tr>`;
   102	  }).join('');
   103	}
   104	export function physFixturesHtml(ev: EvidenceView): string {
   105	  return ev.fixtures.physical.map((f) => `<tr><th scope="row">${esc(f.id)}</th><td>${f.naiveGroupVsDispersedPositive} of ${f.S.length} blocks grouped beats dispersed</td><td>${esc(f.verdict)}; difference-in-differences positive in ${f.B.filter((b) => b > 0).length} of ${f.B.length}</td><td>${f.rejected ? 'rejected' : 'passes'}</td><td>${esc(f.note)}</td></tr>`).join('');
   106	}
   107	export function contrastsHtml(ev: EvidenceView, schedule: string): string {
   108	  const d = ev.fresh.decisions[schedule];
   109	  if (!d) return '';
   110	  const row = (name: string, s: SummaryView) => `<tr><th scope="row">${name}</th><td>${s.positives} of ${s.of}</td><td>${esc(s.p)}</td><td>${s.median2 === null ? 'n/a' : fmt(s.median2 / 2)}</td><td>${s.lower2 === null ? 'n/a' : fmt(s.lower2 / 2)}</td></tr>`;
   111	  return `${row('Grouped minus dispersed, signalling sensor (S)', d.S)}${row('...minus the same for the silent twin (B)', d.B)}${row('...minus the same for the blind drifter', d.Bdrift)}`;
   112	}
   113	export function criteriaHtml(ev: EvidenceView, schedule: string): string {
   114	  const d = ev.fresh.decisions[schedule];
   115	  return d ? d.criteria.map((c) => `<li>${c.pass ? 'Pass' : 'Not met'} (${esc(c.id)}): ${esc(c.detail)}</li>`).join('') : '';
   116	}
   117	export const listHtml = (xs: string[]): string => xs.map((l) => `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`).join('');

    15	      <p class="eyebrow">First rung</p>
    16	      <h1>Does a group find the moving sun better than the same bodies alone?</h1>
    17	      <p>Four bodies sit in a small jar lit by a sun that slides along it. Some can signal in the dark and move away from signal, so a group could, in principle, drift toward the light together. We run the same four bodies packed together and spread apart, in the same jar with the same competition, and ask whether the group does better than the spread-out bodies, beyond what blind walkers, a silent twin and sleepers do. Bodies moving together is not the test: a sun pulling everything the same way looks like teamwork too.</p>
    18	      <p class="disclosure">Exploratory, CPU only, no GPU evidence, mutation off. A local replay, kept off the public site. The page runs no simulation: each choice below shows a separate precomputed history, not a change made to a running world. A negative here means "no interaction benefit detected in this small jar, horizon and schedule set".</p>

     2	
     3	Branch `claude/i08-moving-light` from `origin/main` c4b16ccd. Freeze commit ea254699 (pushed before the run; contains no outcome file). Exploratory, CPU only (sim-ref), mutation off, no physics change.
     4	
     5	## Headline
     6	
     7	**Label: `unsupported-measurement`.** Not "no benefit". The replicate-noise floor of the capture contrast on dev (engine seed only, patches cloned) is delta_cap = 1,429,742 ppm of the audited input energy on the fixed schedule (398,139 paused, 2,592,988 wandering, 2,222,461 reversed), above the frozen 500,000 ppm sensitivity bound (REVIEW.md, deviation 1: a rule added after seeing dev floors, frozen before any fresh seed was read). The sensor did track the sun on dev (sense tracks: true), so the failure is noise, not an inert sensor. The fresh blocks (8700501-8700508) were still run once and are reported in full.
     8	
     9	## What the fresh blocks show (descriptive, not a verdict)
    10	
    11	- Fixed: S positive 5/8, B (against the silent twin) 6/8, B_drift 4/8; bootstrap lower bound of 2*median B = -19,960 ppm; residual coordination above the floor in 1/8 blocks.
    12	- Wandering: S 7/8, B 8/8 (lower bound +754,584 on 2*median), B_drift 4/8, median B_drift negative. Reversed (post-turn window): S 7/8, B 8/8, B_drift 3/8.
    13	- Paused control: B positive 3/8, median B strongly negative; S positive 8/8 (grouping alone helps in a static sun).
    14	- sense-nosig is dynamically the same arm as still (identical readouts in 40/40 dev and noise runs), so B is the contrast against a non-moving arm, and the blind drifter comparator does not agree with it.
    15	- Dispersed cross-talk 958,904 ppm (flagged): the 24 x 16 tile is one signal field; the claim stays "dispersed".
    16	
    17	## Fixtures
    18	
    19	Naive "bodies move together" reads collective motion in common-forcing (fixed, wandering), label-takeover and null-world synthetic fixtures (80-100 percent of pairs agree); the residual control rejects all of them. coupled-synthetic is detected; static-sun is unavailable, not zero. planted-benefit gives `supported` through the full decision, the same table with the benefit planted in paused too gives `inconclusive-confounded`, planted-nothing gives `unsupported-within-tested-domain`. Physical fixtures (blind-drifters, crowding, stationary-growth) are rejected, the first two by identity of comparators (stated).

     1	// Read-only observer (movinglight-metrics-v1). It reads cell arrays, the ledger and RefSim.light at census steps and never touches physics.
     2	// Everything is an integer or an integer-scaled rational: positions in 1/Q cell, errors and variances x1000, ratios in ppm.
     3	import { CH, G, cellCount, ledgerResidual, packLineageLo, stateHash, totalsOf, FLUX_NAMES, type WorldState } from "@bl/schema";
     4	import { RefSim } from "@bl/sim-ref";
     5	import type { Arm } from "./arms.ts";
     6	import { PROTOCOL, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     7	import { circ, meridianPath, scoredFrom } from "./schedule.ts";
     8	
     9	export const METRICS_VERSION = "movinglight-metrics-v1";
    10	
    11	/** One census of an arm, enough to compute every readout over any window. */
    12	export interface Census {
    13	  t: number;
    14	  lightIn: bigint;
    15	  emit: bigint;
    16	  photo: bigint;
    17	  B: number;
    18	  /** Sum over occupied cells of B * signed circular distance to the meridian, and of B * |distance|. */
    19	  sumBd: number;
    20	  sumBabs: number;
    21	  /** Biomass-weighted light at the occupied cells, summed (B * L). */
    22	  sumBL: number;
    23	  /** Cohesion of the lineage centroids about their unwrapped centre: variance (cells^2 x1000), biomass fraction within the radius (ppm); null when they span over W/2. */
    24	  cohVar1000: number | null;
    25	  cohFracPpm: number | null;
    26	  /** Temporal response lag: steps since the meridian last stood at the centroid cell; null when it never did. */
    27	  lag: number | null;
    28	  /** Per body k = 1..n: centroid x in 1/Q cell (mod W*Q) and its biomass, or null when extinct. */
    29	  bodies: ({ q: number; B: number } | null)[];
    30	  residual: bigint;
    31	}
    32	
    33	export interface Series {
    34	  strategy: Arm["strategy"];
    35	  placement: Arm["placement"];
    36	  schedule: Arm["schedule"];
    37	  seed: number;
    38	  inputB: number;
    39	  inputE: number;
    40	  inputEnergy: number;
    41	  censuses: Census[];
    42	  hashes: string[];
    43	  endHash: string;
    44	}
    45	
    46	const modQ = (v: number, WQ: number) => ((v % WQ) + WQ) % WQ;
    47	/** Signed circular difference of two positions in 1/Q cell, in (-WQ/2, WQ/2]. */
    48	export const circQ = (a: number, b: number, WQ: number): number => {
    49	  let d = modQ(a - b, WQ);
    50	  if (d > WQ >> 1) d -= WQ;
    51	  return d;
    52	};
    53	
    54	/** Biomass-weighted unwrapped centroid of a set of cells: anchor = heaviest cell's x, then the mean signed offset. */
    55	function centroidQ(cells: { x: number; B: number }[], W: number, Q: number): number | null {
    56	  if (cells.length === 0) return null;
    57	  let anchor = cells[0];
    58	  for (const c of cells) if (c.B > anchor.B) anchor = c;
    59	  let sb = 0, sbu = 0;
    60	  for (const c of cells) {
    61	    sb += c.B;
    62	    sbu += c.B * circ(c.x, anchor.x, W);
    63	  }
    64	  return modQ(anchor.x * Q + Math.floor((Q * sbu) / sb), W * Q);
    65	}
    66	
    67	export function takeCensus(sim: RefSim, t: number, path: readonly number[], p: Protocol, start: WorldState, totalsStart: { energy: bigint }): Census {
    68	  const s = sim.state;
    69	  const cfg = s.cfg;
    70	  const n = cellCount(cfg), W = p.W, Q = p.Q;
    71	  const m = path[t];
    72	  const byBody: { x: number; B: number }[][] = Array.from({ length: p.n }, () => []);
    73	  let B = 0, sumBd = 0, sumBabs = 0, sumBL = 0;
    74	  const los = Array.from({ length: p.n }, (_, k) => packLineageLo(cfg, k + 1));
    75	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    76	    const b = s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    77	    if (b === 0) continue;
    78	    const x = i % W, y = Math.floor(i / W);
    79	    const d = circ(x, m, W);
    80	    B += b;
    81	    sumBd += b * d;
    82	    sumBabs += b * Math.abs(d);
    83	    sumBL += b * sim.light(x, y, t);
    84	    if (s.genome[G.LIN_HI * n + i] === 0) {
    85	      const k = los.indexOf(s.genome[G.LIN_LO * n + i]);
    86	      if (k >= 0) byBody[k].push({ x, B: b });
    87	    }
    88	  }
    89	  const bodies = byBody.map((cells) => {
    90	    const q = centroidQ(cells, W, Q);
    91	    return q === null ? null : { q, B: cells.reduce((x, c) => x + c.B, 0) };
    92	  });
    93	  // Cohesion of the lineage centroids about an unwrapped centre (anchor = heaviest body); null when they span over W/2.
    94	  let cohVar1000: number | null = null, cohFracPpm: number | null = null, lag: number | null = null;
    95	  const alive = bodies.filter((x): x is { q: number; B: number } => x !== null);
    96	  if (alive.length) {
    97	    let anchor = alive[0];
    98	    for (const b of alive) if (b.B > anchor.B) anchor = b;
    99	    const WQ = W * Q;
   100	    const us = alive.map((b) => ({ u: circQ(b.q, anchor.q, WQ), B: b.B }));
   101	    const lo = Math.min(...us.map((x) => x.u)), hi = Math.max(...us.map((x) => x.u));
   102	    const nbody = us.reduce((a, x) => a + x.B, 0);
   103	    const cQ = Math.floor(us.reduce((a, x) => a + x.B * x.u, 0) / nbody);
   104	    if (hi - lo <= WQ >> 1) {
   105	      let v = 0, within = 0;
   106	      for (const x of us) {
   107	        v += x.B * (x.u - cQ) * (x.u - cQ);
   108	        if (Math.abs(x.u - cQ) <= p.cohesionRadius * Q) within += x.B;
   109	      }
   110	      cohVar1000 = Math.floor((1000 * v) / (nbody * Q * Q));
   111	      cohFracPpm = Math.floor((p.PPM * within) / nbody);
   112	    }
   113	    const cx = modQ(Math.round((anchor.q + cQ) / Q), W);
   114	    for (let u = t; u >= 0; u--)
   115	      if (path[u] === cx) {
   116	        lag = t - u;
   117	        break;
   118	      }
   119	  }
   120	  void start;
   121	  return {
   122	    t,
   123	    lightIn: s.lightIn,
   124	    emit: s.flux[FLUX_NAMES.indexOf("emit")],
   125	    photo: s.flux[FLUX_NAMES.indexOf("photo")],
   126	    B,
   127	    sumBd,
   128	    sumBabs,
   129	    sumBL,
   130	    cohVar1000,
   131	    cohFracPpm,
   132	    lag,
   133	    bodies,
   134	    residual: ledgerResidual(totalsStart, s),
   135	  };
   136	}
   137	
   138	export interface RunOptions {
   139	  /** Instrument the run. false gives the plain twin (state hashes only). */
   140	  observe?: boolean;
   141	  p?: Protocol;
   142	  steps?: number;
   143	}
   144	
   145	/** Run an arm for the horizon, taking a census every p.census steps (and at 0). Conservation must be exactly zero at every census. */
   146	export function runArm(arm: Arm, opts: RunOptions = {}): Series {
   147	  const p = opts.p ?? PROTOCOL;
   148	  const observe = opts.observe ?? true;
   149	  const steps = opts.steps ?? p.T;
   150	  const path = meridianPath(arm.schedule, p);
   151	  // The reversed schedule is scored from its realised turn: take one extra census exactly there.
   152	  const turn = arm.schedule === "reversed" ? scoredFrom("reversed", path, p) : -1;
   153	  const sim = new RefSim(arm.state);
   154	  const totalsStart = { energy: totalsOf(arm.state.cfg, arm.state.cells).energy };
   155	  const censuses: Census[] = [];
   156	  const hashes: string[] = [];
   157	  const start = arm.state;
   158	  if (observe) censuses.push(takeCensus(sim, 0, path, p, start, totalsStart));
   159	  hashes.push(stateHash(sim.state));
   160	  for (let t = 1; t <= steps; t++) {
   161	    sim.step();
   162	    if (t % p.census === 0 || t === turn) {
   163	      hashes.push(stateHash(sim.state));
   164	      if (observe) {
   165	        const c = takeCensus(sim, t, path, p, start, totalsStart);
   166	        if (c.residual !== 0n) throw new Error(`conservation residual ${c.residual} at step ${t}`);
   167	        censuses.push(c);
   168	      }
   169	    }
   170	  }
   171	  return { strategy: arm.strategy, placement: arm.placement, schedule: arm.schedule, seed: arm.seed, inputB: arm.inputB, inputE: arm.inputE, inputEnergy: arm.inputEnergy, censuses, hashes, endHash: stateHash(sim.state) };
   172	}
   173	
   174	const avg = (xs: number[]): number | null => (xs.length ? Math.floor(xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length) : null);
   175	
   176	export interface Window {
   177	  from: number;
   178	  to: number;
   179	}
   180	export const windowOf = (schedule: Arm["schedule"], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Window => ({ from: scoredFrom(schedule, meridianPath(schedule, p), p), to: p.T });
   181	
   182	export interface Readouts {
   183	  window: Window;
   184	  /** Primary capture: lightIn increment over the window per audited inoculum input energy, ppm. */
   185	  capturePpm: number;
   186	  /** Covariates, never primary. */
   187	  exposure1000: number | null;
   188	  emitInc: string;
   189	  photoInc: string;
   190	  /** Tracking: signed bias, absolute circular error (primary), temporal lag; x1000 cells / steps x1000. */
   191	  biasQ1000: number | null;
   192	  absErr1000: number | null;
   193	  lag1000: number | null;
   194	  lagN: number;
   195	  cohVar1000: number | null;
   196	  cohFracPpm: number | null;
   197	  cohN: number;
   198	  /** Survival over the whole run: terminal biomass over the inoculum, ppm; extinct arms keep their terminal outcome. */
   199	  survivalPpm: number;
   200	  extinct: boolean;
   201	  trackedCensuses: number;
   202	}
   203	
   204	export function readouts(r: Series, w: Window, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Readouts {
   205	  const at = (t: number) => {
   206	    const c = r.censuses.find((x) => x.t === t);
   207	    if (!c) throw new Error(`no census at ${t}`);
   208	    return c;
   209	  };
   210	  const a = at(w.from), z = at(w.to);
   211	  const inW = r.censuses.filter((c) => c.t > w.from && c.t <= w.to);
   212	  const live = inW.filter((c) => c.B > 0);
   213	  const capturePpm = Number((BigInt(p.PPM) * (z.lightIn - a.lightIn)) / BigInt(r.inputEnergy));
   214	  const lag = live.filter((c) => c.lag !== null).map((c) => c.lag! * 1000);
   215	  const coh = live.filter((c) => c.cohVar1000 !== null);
   216	  const last = r.censuses[r.censuses.length - 1];
   217	  return {
   218	    window: w,
   219	    capturePpm,
   220	    exposure1000: avg(live.map((c) => Math.floor((1000 * c.sumBL) / c.B))),
   221	    emitInc: `${z.emit - a.emit}`,
   222	    photoInc: `${z.photo - a.photo}`,
   223	    biasQ1000: avg(live.map((c) => Math.floor((1000 * c.sumBd) / c.B))),
   224	    absErr1000: avg(live.map((c) => Math.floor((1000 * c.sumBabs) / c.B))),
   225	    lag1000: avg(lag),
   226	    lagN: lag.length,
   227	    cohVar1000: avg(coh.map((c) => c.cohVar1000!)),
   228	    cohFracPpm: avg(coh.map((c) => c.cohFracPpm!)),
   229	    cohN: coh.length,
   230	    survivalPpm: Math.floor((p.PPM * last.B) / r.inputB),
   231	    extinct: last.B === 0,
   232	    trackedCensuses: live.length,
   233	  };
   234	}
     1	// Residual coordination: the key control. Per census interval and body pair (both lineages alive at both interval ends) the
     2	// idiosyncratic displacement is e_i = dx_i - dm, with dm the sun's recorded displacement for that interval, so sun-driven common forcing
     3	// cancels by construction. A pair's statistic is the covariance of e_i and e_j across the window's intervals (the mean is removed, so a
     4	// shared lag also cancels); the arm statistic is the mean over pairs. Exact integer rationals scaled to milli-Q^2. The naive metric
     5	// (pairwise sign agreement of raw displacements) is computed beside it and saturates under common forcing.
     6	import { PROTOCOL, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     7	import { circQ, type Census } from "./observe.ts";
     8	import { sunDelta } from "./schedule.ts";
     9	
    10	export interface Track {
    11	  /** Positions in 1/Q cell mod W*Q per census; null when the body is extinct at that census. */
    12	  t: number[];
    13	  q: (number | null)[][];
    14	}
    15	
    16	export const trackOf = (censuses: readonly Census[], w: { from: number; to: number }): Track => {
    17	  const cs = censuses.filter((c) => c.t >= w.from && c.t <= w.to);
    18	  const n = cs[0]?.bodies.length ?? 0;
    19	  return { t: cs.map((c) => c.t), q: Array.from({ length: n }, (_, k) => cs.map((c) => c.bodies[k]?.q ?? null)) };
    20	};
    21	
    22	export interface Coordination {
    23	  /** Mean pair covariance of idiosyncratic displacement, milli-Q^2, or null (unavailable, never 0) when no pair has two intervals. */
    24	  covMilliQ2: number | null;
    25	  pairs: number;
    26	  /** Intervals in the window and pair-intervals used. */
    27	  intervals: number;
    28	  pairIntervals: number;
    29	  /** Pair-intervals excluded as label hand-overs (a centroid jump above jumpMaxCells). */
    30	  excluded: number;
    31	  /** Naive metric: fraction of pair-intervals whose raw displacements have the same sign (both nonzero), ppm; null when none. */
    32	  signAgreementPpm: number | null;
    33	}
    34	
    35	export function coordination(tr: Track, path: readonly number[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Coordination {
    36	  const WQ = p.W * p.Q;
    37	  const K = tr.t.length - 1;
    38	  const dm: number[] = [];
    39	  for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) dm.push(sunDelta(path, tr.t[k], tr.t[k + 1], p.W) * p.Q);
    40	  const nb = tr.q.length;
    41	  let moved = 0;
    42	  let excluded = 0, pairs = 0, pairIntervals = 0, agree = 0, both = 0, sum = 0;
    43	  for (let i = 0; i < nb; i++)
    44	    for (let j = i + 1; j < nb; j++) {
    45	      let c = 0, si = 0, sj = 0, sij = 0;
    46	      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
    47	        const a0 = tr.q[i][k], a1 = tr.q[i][k + 1], b0 = tr.q[j][k], b1 = tr.q[j][k + 1];
    48	        if (a0 === null || a1 === null || b0 === null || b1 === null) continue;
    49	        const di = circQ(a1, a0, WQ), dj = circQ(b1, b0, WQ);
    50	        if (Math.abs(di) > p.jumpMaxCells * p.Q || Math.abs(dj) > p.jumpMaxCells * p.Q) {
    51	          excluded++;
    52	          continue;
    53	        }
    54	        if (di !== 0 && dj !== 0) {
    55	          both++;
    56	          if (di > 0 === dj > 0) agree++;
    57	        }
    58	        moved += Math.abs(di) + Math.abs(dj) + Math.abs(dm[k]);
    59	        const ei = di - dm[k], ej = dj - dm[k];
    60	        c++;
    61	        si += ei;
    62	        sj += ej;
    63	        sij += ei * ej;
    64	      }
    65	      if (c < 2) continue;
    66	      pairs++;
    67	      pairIntervals += c;
    68	      sum += Math.floor((1000 * (c * sij - si * sj)) / (c * c));
    69	    }
    70	  return { covMilliQ2: pairs && moved > 0 ? Math.floor(sum / pairs) : null, pairs, intervals: K, pairIntervals, excluded, signAgreementPpm: both ? Math.floor((p.PPM * agree) / both) : null };
    71	}
     1	// Exact integer sun paths. The sweep light is a staircase (rotation floors, wander phase is quantised to 512 bins, amplitude scaling
     2	// floors), so every schedule is frozen as the integer meridian path m(t) the rule produces; "speed" is nominal, the realised mean
     3	// speed, first-move step and turning step are reported. Mirrors packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts `light` (sweep branch).
     4	import { divu, mulu } from "@bl/schema";
     5	import { PROTOCOL, type Protocol, type ScheduleId } from "./protocol.ts";
     6	
     7	/** The meridian cell of the sweep rule at `step` for a config's day/wander keys (the argmax of RefSim.light over x). */
     8	export function meridianAt(cfg: { tileW: number; dayPeriod?: number; wanderPeriod?: number; wanderAmp?: number }, step: number): number {
     9	  const P = cfg.dayPeriod ?? 0;
    10	  let sun = P > 0 ? divu(mulu(step % P, cfg.tileW), P) : 0;
    11	  const WP = cfg.wanderPeriod ?? 0;
    12	  if (WP > 0) {
    13	    const ph = divu(mulu(step % WP, 512), WP);
    14	    const tri = 256 - Math.abs(ph - 256);
    15	    sun = (sun + (((cfg.wanderAmp ?? 0) * tri) >> 8)) % cfg.tileW;
    16	  }
    17	  return sun;
    18	}
    19	
    20	export const keysOf = (schedule: ScheduleId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): { dayPeriod: number; wanderPeriod?: number; wanderAmp?: number } =>
    21	  schedule === "fixed" ? { dayPeriod: p.fixed.P } : schedule === "paused" ? { dayPeriod: 0 } : { dayPeriod: 0, wanderPeriod: p.wander.WP, wanderAmp: p.wander.A };
    22	
    23	/** The meridian cell at steps 0..T inclusive. */
    24	export function meridianPath(schedule: ScheduleId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number[] {
    25	  const k = { tileW: p.W, ...keysOf(schedule, p) };
    26	  return Array.from({ length: p.T + 1 }, (_, t) => meridianAt(k, t));
    27	}
    28	
    29	/** Signed circular distance in (-W/2, W/2]: positive when `x` is east of `m`. */
    30	export function circ(x: number, m: number, W: number): number {
    31	  let d = (((x - m) % W) + W) % W;
    32	  if (d > W >> 1) d -= W;
    33	  return d;
    34	}
    35	
    36	export interface ScheduleReport {
    37	  schedule: ScheduleId;
    38	  keys: ReturnType<typeof keysOf>;
    39	  /** Steps of the path; the path itself is stored so a reader can verify it. */
    40	  pathHash: string;
    41	  firstMoveStep: number | null;
    42	  /** Realised turning step (the first step after which the meridian moves west), or null. */
    43	  turnStep: number | null;
    44	  /** Unwrapped east travel in cells over the first leg and mean speed in cells per 1000 steps (integer). */
    45	  eastLegSteps: number;
    46	  westLegSteps: number;
    47	  meanSpeedPer1000: number;
    48	  /** Realised mean speed over the drifter's 1/64 cell per step, in per-mille. */
    49	  speedRatioPermille: number;
    50	}
    51	
    52	/** Unwrapped sun displacement between steps (positive east), assuming under half a tile per call. */
    53	export const sunDelta = (path: readonly number[], a: number, b: number, W: number): number => {
    54	  let d = 0;
    55	  for (let t = a; t < b; t++) d += circ(path[t + 1], path[t], W);
    56	  return d;
    57	};
    58	
    59	export function reportOf(schedule: ScheduleId, path: readonly number[], hash: string, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): ScheduleReport {
    60	  let first: number | null = null, turn: number | null = null, east = 0, west = 0, total = 0;
    61	  for (let t = 0; t < path.length - 1; t++) {
    62	    const d = circ(path[t + 1], path[t], p.W);
    63	    total += Math.abs(d);
    64	    if (d !== 0 && first === null) first = t;
    65	    if (d < 0 && turn === null) turn = t;
    66	  }
    67	  const steps = path.length - 1;
    68	  // Leg durations in steps: east from the first move to the turn (or the end), west from the turn to the end.
    69	  east = first === null ? 0 : (turn ?? steps) - first;
    70	  west = turn === null ? 0 : steps - turn;
    71	  const perStep1000 = Math.floor((total * 1000) / steps);
    72	  return { schedule, keys: keysOf(schedule, p), pathHash: hash, firstMoveStep: first, turnStep: turn, eastLegSteps: east, westLegSteps: west, meanSpeedPer1000: perStep1000, speedRatioPermille: Math.floor((perStep1000 * 64 * 1000) / 1000) };
    73	}
    74	
    75	/** Start of the scored window: the warm-up end, or for the reversed schedule the realised turning step. */
    76	export const scoredFrom = (schedule: ScheduleId, path: readonly number[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number => {
    77	  if (schedule === "reversed") {
    78	    // The post-turn window starts at the realised turn step itself (observe.ts takes an extra census there), not at the next census.
    79	    for (let t = 0; t < path.length - 1; t++) if (circ(path[t + 1], path[t], p.W) < 0) return t;
    80	  }
    81	  return p.T0;
    82	};

    23	export interface BlockRecord {

    33	export const key = (s: StrategyId, pl: Placement): string => `${s}/${pl}`;

    35	export function summarize(series: Series, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): RunSummary {
    36	  const w = windowOf(series.schedule, p);
    37	  const path = meridianPath(series.schedule, p);
    38	  const pre = series.schedule === "reversed" ? readouts(series, { from: p.T0, to: w.from }, p) : null;
    39	  return {
    40	    strategy: series.strategy,
    41	    placement: series.placement,
    42	    readouts: readouts(series, w, p),
    43	    pre,
    44	    coordination: coordination(trackOf(series.censuses, w), path, p),
    45	    inputB: series.inputB,
    46	    inputE: series.inputE,
    47	    inputEnergy: series.inputEnergy,
    48	    endHash: series.endHash,
    49	    conserved: true,
    50	  };
    51	}

    54	  t: s.censuses.map((c) => c.t),
    55	  d1000: s.censuses.map((c) => (c.B ? Math.floor((c.sumBd * 1000) / c.B) : 0)),

    59	export interface BlockSpec {
    60	  seed: number;
    61	  patchSeed?: number;
    62	  schedules: readonly ScheduleId[];
    63	  strategies: readonly StrategyId[];
    64	  placements?: readonly Placement[];
    65	  withStrip?: boolean;
    66	}
    67	
    68	export function runBlock(spec: BlockSpec, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): BlockRecord {
    69	  const patchSeed = spec.patchSeed ?? spec.seed;
    70	  const patches: Patch[] = buildPatches(p, patchSeed);
    71	  const problems: string[] = [];
    72	  const runs: BlockRecord["runs"] = {};
    73	  const strip: NonNullable<BlockRecord["strip"]> = {};
    74	  for (const sc of spec.schedules) {
    75	    runs[sc] = {};
    76	    for (const st of spec.strategies)
    77	      for (const pl of spec.placements ?? (["grouped", "dispersed"] as const)) {
    78	        const arm = buildArm(st, pl, sc, spec.seed, patches, p);
    79	        problems.push(...auditArm(arm, patches));
    80	        const series = runArm(arm, { p });
    81	        runs[sc][key(st, pl)] = summarize(series, p);
    82	        if (spec.withStrip) strip[`${sc}/${key(st, pl)}`] = stripOf(series, p);
    83	      }
    84	  }
    85	  return {
    86	    seed: spec.seed,
    87	    patchSeed,
    88	    patchAudit: { B: patches.reduce((a, q) => a + q.B, 0), E: patches.reduce((a, q) => a + q.E, 0), problems },
    89	    runs,
    90	    strip: spec.withStrip ? strip : null,
    91	  };
    92	}

    30	export const ARM_EDITS: Record<StrategyId, string> = {
    31	  sense: "darkFleeGenome as is (emits in the dark, moves down the signal gradient)",
    32	  "sense-nosig": "darkFleeGenome with EMIT bias and the EMIT weight from hidden unit 1 zeroed; movement wiring kept (information-free, movement-capable)",
    33	  "sense-nomot": "darkFleeGenome with motility gain 0 (emits, does not move)",
    34	  drift: "sunFollowerGenome (blind east drifter, MX bias 32, gain 12)",
    35	  still: "generalistGenome (motility gain 0)",
    36	};
    37	
    38	export function genomeOf(id: StrategyId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Genome {
    39	  const { defaultMu: mu, defaultSigma: sg } = p;
    40	  if (id === "drift") return sunFollowerGenome(mu, sg);
    41	  if (id === "still") return generalistGenome(mu, sg);
    42	  const g = darkFleeGenome(mu, sg);
    43	  if (id === "sense-nosig") {
    44	    g.weights[W2_OFF + 1 * NN_O + OUT.EMIT] = 0;
    45	    g.weights[B2_OFF + OUT.EMIT] = 0;
    46	  }
    47	  if (id === "sense-nomot") g.motGain = 0;
    48	  return g;
    49	}
    50	export const genomeHash = (id: StrategyId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): string => sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(genomeHex(genomeOf(id, p))));

    52	export interface PatchCell {
    53	  dx: number;
    54	  dy: number;
    55	  B: number;
    56	  E: number;
    57	}
    58	export interface Patch {
    59	  /** Raw lineage id (1..n). */
    60	  raw: number;
    61	  cells: PatchCell[];
    62	  B: number;
    63	  E: number;
    64	}
    65	
    66	/** The n body patches, built once from buildWorld with a fixed seed: discs of the founder rule, noisy biomass, energy proportional. */
    67	export function buildPatches(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, patchSeed = 8_700_000): Patch[] {
    68	  const cfg = worldConfig(patchSeed, "paused", p);
    69	  const W = p.W;
    70	  const founders = Array.from({ length: p.n }, (_, k) => ({
    71	    x: 2 + k * 6,
    72	    y: 8,
    73	    radius: p.body.radius,
    74	    genome: generalistGenome(p.defaultMu, p.defaultSigma),
    75	    biomass: p.body.biomass,
    76	    energy: p.body.energyPerBiomass * p.body.biomass,
    77	  }));
    78	  if (founders[p.n - 1].x + p.body.radius >= W) throw new Error("scratch world too narrow for the patches");
    79	  const s = buildWorld(cfg, { nutrient: 0, founders });
    80	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
    81	  return founders.map((f, k) => {
    82	    const cells: PatchCell[] = [];
    83	    for (let dy = -f.radius; dy <= f.radius; dy++)
    84	      for (let dx = -f.radius; dx <= f.radius; dx++) {
    85	        if (dx * dx + dy * dy > f.radius * f.radius) continue;
    86	        const i = (f.y + dy) * W + (f.x + dx);
    87	        const B = s.cells[CH.B * n + i];
    88	        if (B > 0) cells.push({ dx, dy, B, E: s.cells[CH.E * n + i] });
    89	      }
    90	    return { raw: k + 1, cells, B: cells.reduce((a, c) => a + c.B, 0), E: cells.reduce((a, c) => a + c.E, 0) };
    91	  });
    92	}
    93	
    94	export interface Position {
    95	  x: number;
    96	  y: number;
    97	}
    98	/** Centres of the n bodies. Grouped: a compact 2 x 2 block; dispersed: the maximal spacing; both centred `offset` cells west of the t=0 meridian. */
    99	export function positions(placement: Placement, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Position[] {
   100	  const cx = (((0 - p.placement.offset) % p.W) + p.W) % p.W;
   101	  const g = p.placement.groupedSpacing;
   102	  if (placement === "grouped") {
   103	    const r = Math.floor(p.n / 2) || 1;
   104	    return Array.from({ length: p.n }, (_, k) => ({
   105	      x: (((cx + (k % r) * g - Math.floor(((r - 1) * g) / 2)) % p.W) + p.W) % p.W,
   106	      y: p.placement.groupedY + Math.floor(k / r) * g - Math.floor(g / 2),
   107	    }));
   108	  }
   109	  const r = Math.floor(p.n / 2) || 1;
   110	  const dx = Math.floor(p.W / r);
   111	  return Array.from({ length: p.n }, (_, k) => ({
   112	    x: (((cx + (k % r) * dx - Math.floor(((r - 1) * dx) / 2)) % p.W) + p.W) % p.W,
   113	    y: p.placement.dispersedY[Math.floor(k / r) % p.placement.dispersedY.length],
   114	  }));
   115	}

   117	export interface Arm {
   118	  strategy: StrategyId;
   119	  placement: Placement;
   120	  schedule: ScheduleId;
   121	  seed: number;
   122	  state: WorldState;
   123	  /** Audited input: biomass B, free energy E in bodies, and the inoculum input energy E + eB * B. */
   124	  inputB: number;
   125	  inputE: number;
   126	  inputEnergy: number;
   127	}
   128	
   129	/** One arm: nutrient everywhere, the patches stamped (copied by translation) at the placement's centres with the strategy's genome. */
   130	export function buildArm(strategy: StrategyId, placement: Placement, schedule: ScheduleId, seed: number, patches: Patch[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, positionsOverride?: Position[]): Arm {
   131	  const cfg = worldConfig(seed, schedule, p);
   132	  const s = allocState(cfg);
   133	  const n = cellCount(cfg);
   134	  s.cells.fill(p.nutrient, CH.A * n, (CH.A + 1) * n);
   135	  s.cells.fill(MOT_ZERO, CH.MOT * n, (CH.MOT + 1) * n);
   136	  const pos = positionsOverride ?? positions(placement, p);
   137	  const g = genomeOf(strategy, p);
   138	  let inputB = 0, inputE = 0;
   139	  patches.forEach((patch, k) => {
   140	    const words = encodeGenome(g, 0, packLineageLo(cfg, patch.raw));
   141	    for (const c of patch.cells) {
   142	      const x = (((pos[k].x + c.dx) % p.W) + p.W) % p.W;
   143	      const y = (((pos[k].y + c.dy) % p.H) + p.H) % p.H;
   144	      const i = y * p.W + x;
   145	      if (s.cells[CH.B * n + i] !== 0) throw new Error(`patches overlap at cell ${i}`);
   146	      s.cells[CH.B * n + i] = c.B;
   147	      s.cells[CH.E * n + i] = c.E;
   148	      for (let w = 0; w < words.length; w++) s.genome[w * n + i] = words[w];
   149	      inputB += c.B;
   150	      inputE += c.E;
   151	    }
   152	  });
   153	  const errs = validateState(s);
   154	  if (errs.length) throw new Error(`invalid arm: ${errs[0]}`);
   155	  return { strategy, placement, schedule, seed, state: s, inputB, inputE, inputEnergy: inputE + cfg.eB * inputB };
   156	}
   157	
   158	/** Audit of a built arm against the patches: totals equal the patch sums. */
   159	export function auditArm(a: Arm, patches: Patch[]): string[] {
   160	  const out: string[] = [];
   161	  const B = patches.reduce((x, q) => x + q.B, 0), E = patches.reduce((x, q) => x + q.E, 0);
   162	  if (a.inputB !== B) out.push(`arm ${a.strategy}/${a.placement}: B ${a.inputB} != patches ${B}`);
   163	  if (a.inputE !== E) out.push(`arm ${a.strategy}/${a.placement}: E ${a.inputE} != patches ${E}`);
   164	  const t = totalsOf(a.state.cfg, a.state.cells);
   165	  if (t.B !== BigInt(B)) out.push("state B total differs from the patches");
   166	  void G; void IN;
   167	  return out;
   168	}

     1	// Fixtures, built before any search or fresh run. Each is a case where the naive metric ("bodies move together and stay near the sun":
     2	// pairwise sign agreement of raw displacement, group tracking lag) reads collective motion while the residual-coordination control and
     3	// the complete decision function must reject it. Synthetic fixtures generate tracks directly (integer noise from lowbias32); physical
     4	// fixtures run sim-ref worlds. Pure and deterministic.
     5	import { lowbias32 } from "@bl/schema";
     6	import { runBlock } from "./assay.ts";
     7	import { coordination, type Coordination, type Track } from "./coordination.ts";
     8	import { decideSchedule, medianTimes2, verdictOf, type BlockNumbers, type Floors, type Verdict } from "./decision.ts";
     9	import { PROTOCOL, type Protocol, type ScheduleId } from "./protocol.ts";

    12	export const NAIVE_SIGN_PPM = 800_000;
    13	const rnd = (seed: number, a: number, b: number, span: number): number => (lowbias32(Math.imul(seed, 0x9e3779b1) ^ Math.imul(a, 7919) ^ Math.imul(b, 104729)) % (2 * span + 1)) - span;

    15	export type SyntheticKind = "common-forcing" | "coupled-synthetic" | "label-takeover" | "static-sun" | "null-world";
    16	
    17	export interface SyntheticOpts {
    18	  noiseQ: number;
    19	  /** Amplitude (1/Q cell per interval) of the shared group fluctuation in the coupled case. */
    20	  couplingQ: number;
    21	}
    22	export const SYNTH: SyntheticOpts = { noiseQ: 24, couplingQ: 60 };
    23	
    24	/** Synthetic tracks of the n bodies over the censuses [T0, T]: positions in 1/Q cell mod W*Q. */
    25	export function syntheticTracks(kind: SyntheticKind, placement: "grouped" | "dispersed", seed: number, schedule: ScheduleId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, o: SyntheticOpts = SYNTH): Track {
    26	  const WQ = p.W * p.Q;
    27	  const path = meridianPath(kind === "static-sun" ? "paused" : schedule, p);
    28	  const ts: number[] = [];
    29	  for (let t = p.T0; t <= p.T; t += p.census) ts.push(t);
    30	  const spread = placement === "grouped" ? 3 : 12;
    31	  const q: (number | null)[][] = [];
    32	  for (let i = 0; i < p.n; i++) {
    33	    const row: number[] = [];
    34	    let pos = (((p.W - p.placement.offset) % p.W) + i * spread) * p.Q;
    35	    for (let k = 0; k < ts.length; k++) {
    36	      if (k > 0) {
    37	        const dm = sunDelta(path, ts[k - 1], ts[k], p.W) * p.Q;
    38	        const own = rnd(seed, i + 1, k, o.noiseQ);
    39	        const group = kind === "coupled-synthetic" && placement === "grouped" ? rnd(seed, 99, k, o.couplingQ) : 0;
    40	        if (kind === "static-sun") pos += 0;
    41	        else if (kind === "null-world") pos += dm;
    42	        else if (kind === "label-takeover") {
    43	          // Material follows the sun; at every fourth interval the tags of two bodies swap hands (a jump with no material displacement).
    44	          pos += dm + own + (k % 4 === 0 ? ((i % 2 ? -1 : 1) * 3 * p.W * p.Q) / 4 : 0);
    45	        } else pos += dm + own + group;
    46	      }
    47	      row.push(((pos % WQ) + WQ) % WQ);
    48	    }
    49	    q.push(row);
    50	  }
    51	  return { t: ts, q };
    52	}

    54	export interface CoordFixture {
    55	  id: string;
    56	  schedule: ScheduleId;
    57	  seeds: number[];
    58	  /** Naive reading in the grouped arm: pairwise sign agreement (ppm) per seed, and whether it reads "collective motion". */
    59	  naiveGroupedPpm: (number | null)[];
    60	  naiveReads: boolean;
    61	  /** Residual coordination (grouped minus dispersed, milli-Q^2) per seed, null when unavailable. */
    62	  residual: (number | null)[];
    63	  excluded: number;
    64	  floor: number;
    65	  /** 'coordination-detected' (a readout label) or 'unsupported-within-tested-domain'. */
    66	  label: "coordination-detected" | "unsupported-within-tested-domain" | "unavailable";
    67	  rejected: boolean;
    68	}

    70	/** The synthetic floor: the largest |grouped - dispersed| of the uncoupled common-forcing fixture on the replicate-noise seeds. */
    71	export function syntheticFloor(schedule: ScheduleId, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): number {
    72	  const path = meridianPath(schedule, p);
    73	  let f = 0;
    74	  for (const s of p.noiseSeeds) {
    75	    const g = coordination(syntheticTracks("common-forcing", "grouped", s, schedule, p), path, p).covMilliQ2;
    76	    const d = coordination(syntheticTracks("common-forcing", "dispersed", s, schedule, p), path, p).covMilliQ2;
    77	    f = Math.max(f, Math.abs((g ?? 0) - (d ?? 0)));
    78	  }
    79	  return f;
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function coordFixture(kind: SyntheticKind, schedule: ScheduleId, floor: number, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): CoordFixture {
    83	  const path = meridianPath(kind === "static-sun" ? "paused" : schedule, p);
    84	  const seeds = [...p.fixtureSeeds];
    85	  const g: Coordination[] = [], d: Coordination[] = [];
    86	  for (const s of seeds) {
    87	    g.push(coordination(syntheticTracks(kind, "grouped", s, schedule, p), path, p));
    88	    d.push(coordination(syntheticTracks(kind, "dispersed", s, schedule, p), path, p));
    89	  }
    90	  const residual = g.map((c, i) => (c.covMilliQ2 === null || d[i].covMilliQ2 === null ? null : c.covMilliQ2 - d[i].covMilliQ2!));
    91	  const avail = residual.filter((x): x is number => x !== null);
    92	  const above = avail.filter((x) => x > floor).length;
    93	  const naive = g.map((c) => c.signAgreementPpm);
    94	  const naiveReads = naive.filter((x) => x !== null && x >= NAIVE_SIGN_PPM).length >= p.rule.residualMin;
    95	  const label = avail.length === 0 ? "unavailable" : above >= p.rule.residualMin ? "coordination-detected" : "unsupported-within-tested-domain";
    96	  return { id: kind, schedule, seeds, naiveGroupedPpm: naive, naiveReads, residual, excluded: g.reduce((a, c) => a + c.excluded, 0), floor, label, rejected: label !== "coordination-detected" };
    97	}

    99	/** A synthetic block table for the complete decision function: a planted positive B (sense benefits grouped, others do not). */
   100	export function plantedTable(planted: { fixed: number; paused: number; reversed: number; wandering: number }, seed: number): Record<string, BlockNumbers[]> {
   101	  const out: Record<string, BlockNumbers[]> = {};
   102	  for (const sc of ["fixed", "paused", "reversed", "wandering"] as const) {
   103	    out[sc] = [];
   104	    for (let b = 0; b < 8; b++) {
   105	      const noise = (k: number) => rnd(seed, b * 8 + k, sc.length, 4000);
   106	      const cn = (k: number) => rnd(seed, b * 8 + k, sc.length + 17, 400);
   107	      const arm = (g: number, dsp: number, covG: number, covD: number) => ({ grouped: { capturePpm: 300_000 + g, survivalPpm: 600_000, cov: covG }, dispersed: { capturePpm: 300_000 + dsp, survivalPpm: 600_000, cov: covD } });
   108	      out[sc].push({
   109	        sense: arm(planted[sc] + noise(1), noise(2), 6000 + cn(3), cn(4)),
   110	        "sense-nosig": arm(noise(5), noise(6), cn(7), cn(8)),
   111	        "sense-nomot": arm(noise(9), noise(10), cn(11), cn(12)),
   112	        drift: arm(noise(13), noise(14), cn(15), cn(16)),
   113	        still: arm(noise(17), noise(18), cn(19), cn(20)),
   114	      });
   115	    }
   116	  }
   117	  return out;
   118	}

   134	  naiveSignAgreementPpm: (number | null)[];
   135	  naiveGroupVsDispersedPositive: number;

   147	export const PHYS_STRATEGIES = ["drift", "sense-nosig", "still"] as const;

   149	/** Physical fixtures from sim-ref worlds on the fixture seeds (fixed schedule): blind drifters, crowding, stationary growth. */
   150	export function physicalFixtures(floors: Floors, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL, blocks = PROTOCOL.fixtureSeeds.map((seed) => runBlock({ seed, schedules: ["fixed"], strategies: PHYS_STRATEGIES }, p))): PhysicalFixture[] {
   151	  const sc = "fixed";
   152	  const num = (b: (typeof blocks)[number], st: string, pl: "grouped" | "dispersed") => b.runs[sc][`${st}/${pl}`];
   153	  const contrast = (st: string) => blocks.map((b) => num(b, st, "grouped").readouts.capturePpm - num(b, st, "dispersed").readouts.capturePpm);
   154	  const cov = (st: string) => blocks.map((b) => { const g = num(b, st, "grouped").coordination.covMilliQ2, d = num(b, st, "dispersed").coordination.covMilliQ2; return g === null || d === null ? null : g - d; });
   155	  const sign = (st: string) => blocks.map((b) => num(b, st, "grouped").coordination.signAgreementPpm);
   156	  const Sn = contrast("sense-nosig");
   157	  const mk = (id: string, cand: string, comparator: string, note: string): PhysicalFixture => {
   158	    const S = contrast(cand), C = contrast(comparator);
   159	    const B = S.map((x, i) => x - C[i]);
   160	    const residual = cov(cand);
   161	    const aboveFloor = residual.filter((x) => x !== null && x > floors.res).length;
   162	    const m = medianTimes2(B);
   163	    const decided = !(m !== null && m > 2 * floors.cap && B.filter((x) => x > 0).length >= p.rule.minPositive);
   164	    return { id, naiveSignAgreementPpm: sign(cand), naiveGroupVsDispersedPositive: S.filter((x) => x > 0).length, S, B, residual, verdict: aboveFloor >= p.rule.residualMin ? "residual-above-floor" : "residual-within-floor", residualWithinFloor: aboveFloor < p.rule.residualMin, rejected: decided && (id !== "stationary-growth" || aboveFloor < p.rule.residualMin), note };
   165	  };
   166	  void Sn;
   167	  return [
   168	    mk("blind-drifters-physical", "drift", "drift", "the hard-coded heading is its own comparator, so B is zero by identity (a rejection by construction, not a discovery); any grouped-versus-dispersed difference is crowding"),
   169	    mk("crowding-physical", "still", "sense-nosig", "no useful signalling and no movement: grouped and dispersed still differ (crowding, shared field); the comparator sense-nosig is dynamically the same arm (REVIEW.md), so B is zero by identity, not by rejection"),
   170	    mk("stationary-growth", "still", "sense-nosig", "no active motility: lineage centroids move by growth and transport only, residual coordination should stay within the floor"),
   171	  ];
   172	}
     1	// Pre-stated decision rules (PLAN section 6), pure integer arithmetic. A schedule's contrast family comes from the capture ppm of the five
     2	// strategies in grouped and dispersed placement across the blocks. Nothing here reads a fresh outcome to choose a rule.
     3	import { lowbias32 } from "@bl/schema";
     4	import { PROTOCOL, type Protocol } from "./protocol.ts";
     5	
     6	export interface ArmNumbers {
     7	  capturePpm: number;
     8	  survivalPpm: number;
     9	  /** Residual-coordination statistic (milli-Q^2) or null when unavailable. */
    10	  cov: number | null;
    11	}
    12	/** One block's numbers for one schedule: strategy -> placement -> numbers. Missing strategies make the block incomplete. */
    13	export type BlockNumbers = Partial<Record<string, { grouped: ArmNumbers; dispersed: ArmNumbers }>>;
    14	
    15	export interface Floors {
    16	  cap: number;
    17	  res: number;
    18	}
    19	
    20	export const medianTimes2 = (xs: readonly number[]): number | null => {
    21	  if (!xs.length) return null;
    22	  const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    23	  const m = s.length >> 1;
    24	  return s.length % 2 ? 2 * s[m] : s[m - 1] + s[m];
    25	};
    26	
    27	/** Exact one-sided sign-test tail P(X >= k), X ~ Binomial(n, 1/2), as an integer numerator over 2^n. */
    28	export function signTail(k: number, n: number): { num: number; den: number } {
    29	  let num = 0;
    30	  let c = 1;
    31	  for (let j = 0; j <= n; j++) {
    32	    if (j >= k) num += c;
    33	    c = (c * (n - j)) / (j + 1);
    34	  }
    35	  return { num, den: 2 ** n };
    36	}
    37	export const signP = (k: number, n: number): string => {
    38	  const { num, den } = signTail(k, n);
    39	  return `${num}/${den}`;
    40	};
    41	
    42	/** Deterministic bootstrap of the median (as twice-median): resamples whole blocks with lowbias32, returns the level quantile. */
    43	export function bootstrapLowerBound2(xs: readonly number[], draws: number, seed: number, levelPpm: number): number | null {
    44	  if (!xs.length) return null;
    45	  const meds: number[] = [];
    46	  let state = lowbias32(seed);
    47	  for (let d = 0; d < draws; d++) {
    48	    const pick: number[] = [];
    49	    for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) {
    50	      state = lowbias32((state + 0x9e3779b9) >>> 0);
    51	      pick.push(xs[state % xs.length]);
    52	    }
    53	    meds.push(medianTimes2(pick)!);
    54	  }
    55	  meds.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    56	  return meds[Math.floor((meds.length * levelPpm) / 1_000_000)];
    57	}
    58	
    59	export interface Contrasts {
    60	  S: number;
    61	  N: number;
    62	  V: number;
    63	  B: number;
    64	  Bdrift: number;
    65	  /** Residual coordination (grouped minus dispersed) of sense and of the no-active-motility arms; null when unavailable. */
    66	  resSense: number | null;
    67	  resStill: number | null;
    68	  resNomot: number | null;
    69	  survDiff: number;
    70	}
    71	
    72	const diff = (a: number | null, b: number | null) => (a === null || b === null ? null : a - b);
    73	export function contrastsOf(b: BlockNumbers): Contrasts | null {
    74	  const s = b["sense"], n = b["sense-nosig"], d = b["drift"], st = b["still"], nm = b["sense-nomot"];
    75	  if (!s || !n || !d || !st || !nm) return null;
    76	  const S = s.grouped.capturePpm - s.dispersed.capturePpm;
    77	  const N = n.grouped.capturePpm - n.dispersed.capturePpm;
    78	  const V = d.grouped.capturePpm - d.dispersed.capturePpm;
    79	  return {
    80	    S,
    81	    N,
    82	    V,
    83	    B: S - N,
    84	    Bdrift: S - V,
    85	    resSense: diff(s.grouped.cov, s.dispersed.cov),
    86	    resStill: diff(st.grouped.cov, st.dispersed.cov),
    87	    resNomot: diff(nm.grouped.cov, nm.dispersed.cov),
    88	    survDiff: s.grouped.survivalPpm - s.dispersed.survivalPpm,
    89	  };
    90	}
    91	
    92	export interface Criterion {
    93	  id: string;
    94	  pass: boolean;
    95	  detail: string;
    96	}
    97	export interface Summary {
    98	  positives: number;
    99	  of: number;
   100	  p: string;
   101	  median2: number | null;
   102	  lower2: number | null;
   103	}
   104	export interface ScheduleDecision {
   105	  blocks: number;
   106	  complete: number;
   107	  contrasts: (Contrasts | null)[];
   108	  S: Summary;
   109	  B: Summary;
   110	  Bdrift: Summary;
   111	  criteria: Criterion[];
   112	  /** (i)-(iii) hold (the persistence test used for transfer schedules). */
   113	  core: boolean;
   114	  /** Estimates are not positive: median S or median B at or below zero. */
   115	  nonPositive: boolean;
   116	  /** Comparators disagree: one of B and B_drift has a median clearly above the floor while the other is at or below zero. */
   117	  comparatorsDisagree: boolean;
   118	}
   119	
   120	function summary(xs: number[], p: Protocol): Summary {
   121	  const k = xs.filter((x) => x > 0).length;
   122	  return { positives: k, of: xs.length, p: signP(k, xs.length), median2: medianTimes2(xs), lower2: bootstrapLowerBound2(xs, p.rule.bootstrapDraws, p.rule.bootstrapSeed, p.rule.bootstrapLevelPpm) };
   123	}
   124	
   125	/** Decide one schedule from its block numbers. `full` also checks (iv)-(vi); transfer schedules use the core (i)-(iii) only. */
   126	export function decideSchedule(blocks: readonly BlockNumbers[], floors: Floors, full: boolean, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): ScheduleDecision {
   127	  // The frozen block count is the denominator: fewer supplied blocks count as missing, never as a smaller panel.
   128	  const total = Math.max(blocks.length, p.rule.blocks);
   129	  const cs = [...blocks.map(contrastsOf), ...Array.from({ length: total - blocks.length }, () => null)];
   130	  const ok = cs.filter((c): c is Contrasts => c !== null);
   131	  const need = p.rule.minPositive;
   132	  const S = summary(ok.map((c) => c.S), p), B = summary(ok.map((c) => c.B), p), Bd = summary(ok.map((c) => c.Bdrift), p);
   133	  const criteria: Criterion[] = [];
   134	  const add = (id: string, pass: boolean, detail: string) => criteria.push({ id, pass, detail });
   135	  // Missing blocks make the rule fail, never drop: positives are counted against the full block count.
   136	  add("i", S.positives >= need && B.positives >= need && ok.length === total, `S positive ${S.positives}/${total} (p ${S.p}), B positive ${B.positives}/${total} (p ${B.p}), need ${need}`);
   137	  add("ii", S.median2 !== null && B.median2 !== null && S.median2 > 2 * floors.cap && B.median2 > 2 * floors.cap, `2*median S ${S.median2}, 2*median B ${B.median2}, 2*floor ${2 * floors.cap}`);
   138	  add("iii", B.lower2 !== null && B.lower2 > 0, `bootstrap lower bound of 2*median B ${B.lower2}`);
   139	  if (full) {
   140	    const avail = ok.filter((c) => c.resSense !== null);
   141	    const resPos = ok.filter((c) => c.resSense !== null && c.resSense > floors.res).length;
   142	    const unavailable = total - avail.length;
   143	    const inside = (key: "resStill" | "resNomot") => ok.filter((c) => c[key] !== null && Math.abs(c[key]!) <= floors.res).length;
   144	    add("iv", unavailable <= 2 && resPos >= p.rule.residualMin && inside("resStill") >= p.rule.residualMin && inside("resNomot") >= p.rule.residualMin, `residual above floor ${resPos}/${total} (need ${p.rule.residualMin}), unavailable ${unavailable}, still inside ${inside("resStill")}, nomot inside ${inside("resNomot")}`);
   145	    // (v): grouped sense survival must not be below dispersed sense survival by more than the margin, in the median and in at least residualMin of the blocks.
   146	    const sm = medianTimes2(ok.map((c) => c.survDiff));
   147	    const survOk = ok.filter((c) => c.survDiff >= -p.survivalMarginPpm).length;
   148	    add("v", sm !== null && sm >= -2 * p.survivalMarginPpm && survOk >= p.rule.residualMin, `2*median survival difference (grouped minus dispersed sense) ${sm}, margin ${-2 * p.survivalMarginPpm}; within the margin in ${survOk}/${total} blocks (need ${p.rule.residualMin})`);
   149	    add("vi", Bd.median2 !== null && Bd.median2 > 2 * floors.cap, `2*median B_drift ${Bd.median2}`);
   150	  }
   151	  return {
   152	    blocks: total,
   153	    complete: ok.length,
   154	    contrasts: cs,
   155	    S,
   156	    B,
   157	    Bdrift: Bd,
   158	    criteria,
   159	    core: criteria.filter((c) => ["i", "ii", "iii"].includes(c.id)).every((c) => c.pass),
   160	    // Not positive: a median at or below zero with at most half the blocks positive. A median tie at zero with a positive majority is inconclusive, not unsupported.
   161	    nonPositive: ((S.median2 ?? 0) <= 0 && S.positives * 2 <= total) || ((B.median2 ?? 0) <= 0 && B.positives * 2 <= total),
   162	    // One comparator shows a benefit above the floor while the other shows none.
   163	    comparatorsDisagree: ((B.median2 ?? 0) > 2 * floors.cap && (Bd.median2 ?? 0) <= 0) || ((Bd.median2 ?? 0) > 2 * floors.cap && (B.median2 ?? 0) <= 0),
   164	  };
   165	}
   166	
   167	export const LABELS = ["supported", "unsupported-within-tested-domain", "inconclusive-confounded", "inconclusive", "invalid-or-incomplete", "unsupported-measurement"] as const;
   168	export type Label = (typeof LABELS)[number];
   169	
   170	export interface Verdict {
   171	  label: Label;
   172	  reasons: string[];
   173	  schedules: Record<string, { core: boolean; allCriteria: boolean }>;
   174	}
   175	
   176	export interface VerdictInput {
   177	  fixed: ScheduleDecision;
   178	  paused: ScheduleDecision | null;
   179	  reversed: ScheduleDecision | null;
   180	  wandering: ScheduleDecision | null;
   181	  /** Floors per schedule (the paused control is judged against its own floor). */
   182	  floors: Record<string, Floors>;
   183	  /** Calibration found the assay insensitive (sense does not track the sun on dev, or the capture noise floor exceeds the frozen bound). */
   184	  insensitive: boolean;
   185	  /** Blocks with a failed audit or missing engine data. */
   186	  invalidBlocks: number;
   187	  blocks: number;
   188	}
   189	
   190	/** The overall label, following the pre-stated rules in PLAN section 6. */
   191	export function verdictOf(v: VerdictInput): Verdict {
   192	  const sched: Verdict["schedules"] = {};
   193	  for (const [id, d] of [["fixed", v.fixed], ["paused", v.paused], ["reversed", v.reversed], ["wandering", v.wandering]] as const)
   194	    if (d) sched[id] = { core: d.core, allCriteria: d.criteria.every((c) => c.pass) };
   195	  const done = (label: Label, why: string): Verdict => ({ label, reasons: [why], schedules: sched });
   196	  if (v.invalidBlocks > 1 || v.blocks - v.fixed.complete > 1) return done("invalid-or-incomplete", `${v.invalidBlocks} invalid blocks, ${v.blocks - v.fixed.complete} incomplete in the screen`);
   197	  if (v.insensitive) return done("unsupported-measurement", "dev calibration: the assay is insensitive (sense does not track the sun at the matched speed, or the replicate-noise floor of the capture readout exceeds the frozen bound)");
   198	  const f = v.fixed;
   199	  if (!f.criteria.every((c) => c.pass)) {
   200	    const failed = f.criteria.filter((c) => !c.pass).map((c) => c.id).join(", ");
   201	    if (f.comparatorsDisagree) return done("inconclusive-confounded", `screen: the information-free and hard-coded comparators disagree (failed ${failed})`);
   202	    if (f.nonPositive) return done("unsupported-within-tested-domain", `screen: median S or median B is not positive (failed ${failed})`);
   203	    return done("inconclusive", `screen: estimates are positive but ${failed} failed`);
   204	  }
   205	  const pausedBenefit = v.paused === null || v.paused.B.median2 === null || Math.abs(v.paused.B.median2) > 2 * v.floors.paused.cap;
   206	  if (pausedBenefit) return done("inconclusive-confounded", "paused (static) control shows a benefit or was not run");
   207	  for (const id of ["reversed", "wandering"] as const) {
   208	    const d = v[id];
   209	    if (!d) return done("inconclusive", `transfer ${id} not run`);
   210	    if (!d.core) return done(d.nonPositive ? "unsupported-within-tested-domain" : "inconclusive", `transfer ${id}: benefit does not persist (${d.criteria.filter((c) => !c.pass).map((c) => c.id).join(", ")} failed)`);
   211	  }
   212	  return done("supported", "screening and both transfer schedules pass; the paused control is within the floor");
   213	}

codex
The page clearly labels schedule choices as separate replay histories, and its deployment exclusions mirror `/niche/`. A concern has emerged in the synthetic fixtures: grouped and dispersed tracks appear to reuse identical noise, which could force a zero residual without testing whether common forcing is removed.

    13	/** Runs a batch of single-schedule block jobs; the tool supplies a two-process pool, tests and replay supply this sequential one. */
    14	export type JobRunner = (specs: BlockSpec[]) => Promise<BlockRecord[]>;
    15	export const sequential: JobRunner = async (specs) => specs.map((s) => runBlock(s));

    17	const hashOf = (v: unknown) => sha256HexJS(new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJSON(v)));
    18	const ALL: readonly StrategyId[] = PROTOCOL.strategies as StrategyId[];

    20	/** Merge single-schedule jobs of the same seed into one block record (job order is the schedule order). */
    21	export function mergeBlocks(records: readonly BlockRecord[]): BlockRecord[] {
    22	  const out = new Map<number, BlockRecord>();
    23	  for (const r of records) {
    24	    const cur = out.get(r.seed);
    25	    if (!cur) out.set(r.seed, { ...r, runs: { ...r.runs }, strip: r.strip ? { ...r.strip } : null, patchAudit: { ...r.patchAudit, problems: [...r.patchAudit.problems] } });
    26	    else {
    27	      Object.assign(cur.runs, r.runs);
    28	      if (r.strip) cur.strip = { ...(cur.strip ?? {}), ...r.strip };
    29	      cur.patchAudit.problems.push(...r.patchAudit.problems);
    30	    }
    31	  }
    32	  return [...out.values()];
    33	}
    34	
    35	export const jobsFor = (seeds: readonly number[], schedules: readonly ScheduleId[], strategies: readonly StrategyId[], opts: { patchSeed?: number; stripSeed?: number } = {}): BlockSpec[] =>
    36	  seeds.flatMap((seed) => schedules.map((schedule) => ({ seed, patchSeed: opts.patchSeed ?? seed, schedules: [schedule], strategies, withStrip: seed === opts.stripSeed })));
    37	
    38	/** BlockNumbers of one schedule from a merged block. */
    39	export function numbersOf(b: BlockRecord, schedule: ScheduleId): BlockNumbers {
    40	  const out: BlockNumbers = {};
    41	  for (const st of ALL) {
    42	    const g = b.runs[schedule]?.[`${st}/grouped`], d = b.runs[schedule]?.[`${st}/dispersed`];
    43	    if (!g || !d) continue;
    44	    out[st] = {
    45	      grouped: { capturePpm: g.readouts.capturePpm, survivalPpm: g.readouts.survivalPpm, cov: g.coordination.covMilliQ2 },
    46	      dispersed: { capturePpm: d.readouts.capturePpm, survivalPpm: d.readouts.survivalPpm, cov: d.coordination.covMilliQ2 },
    47	    };
    48	  }
    49	  return out;
    50	}
