    75	export interface Workload {
    76	  formula: string;
    77	  worldsFresh: number;
    78	  worldsFixtures: number;
    79	  steps: number;
    80	  maxProcesses: 2;
    81	}
    82	
    83	export function workload(p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Workload {
    84	  const worldsFresh = p.freshSeeds.length * p.schedules.length * p.strategies.length * 2;
    85	  const worldsFixtures = p.fixtureSeeds.length * 3 * 2;
    86	  return { formula: "fresh seeds * schedules * strategies * 2 placements + fixture seeds * 3 strategies * 2 placements, each T steps", worldsFresh, worldsFixtures, steps: (worldsFresh + worldsFixtures) * p.T, maxProcesses: 2 };
    87	}
    88	
    89	const spanOver = (xs: readonly number[]): number => {
    90	  let m = 0;
    91	  for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < xs.length; j++) m = Math.max(m, Math.abs(xs[i] - xs[j]));
    92	  return m;
    93	};
    94	
    95	/** Floors from replicate-noise blocks (cloned patches, engine seed only): the largest spread of each contrast and residual across seeds. */
    96	export function floorsFromNoise(blocks: readonly BlockRecord[], schedule: ScheduleId): Floors {
    97	  const cs = blocks.map((b) => contrastsOf(numbersOf(b, schedule))).filter((c) => c !== null);
    98	  const caps = [cs.map((c) => c!.S), cs.map((c) => c!.N), cs.map((c) => c!.V), cs.map((c) => c!.B), cs.map((c) => c!.Bdrift)];
    99	  const res = [cs.map((c) => c!.resSense), cs.map((c) => c!.resStill), cs.map((c) => c!.resNomot)].map((xs) => xs.filter((x): x is number => x !== null));
   100	  return { cap: Math.max(0, ...caps.map(spanOver)), res: Math.max(0, ...res.map(spanOver)) };
   101	}
   102	
   103	export function crossTalkOf(placement: "grouped" | "dispersed", p: Protocol, seed: number): CrossTalk {
   104	  const patches = buildPatches(p, seed);
   105	  const pos = positions(placement, p);
   106	  const run = (bodies: number) => {
   107	    const arm = buildArm("sense", placement, "fixed", seed, patches.slice(0, bodies), p, pos.slice(0, bodies));
   108	    const sim = new RefSim(arm.state);
   109	    for (let t = 0; t < p.T; t++) sim.step();
   110	    return sim.state;
   111	  };
   112	  const all = run(p.n), solo = run(1);
   113	  const n = cellCount(all.cfg);
   114	  // Signal in a disc of radius 3 around body 0's start cell.
   115	  const c = pos[0];
   116	  const R = 3;
   117	  let a = 0, s = 0, cells = 0;
   118	  for (let dy = -R; dy <= R; dy++)
   119	    for (let dx = -R; dx <= R; dx++) {
   120	      if (dx * dx + dy * dy > R * R) continue;
   121	      const i = ((((c.y + dy) % p.H) + p.H) % p.H) * p.W + ((((c.x + dx) % p.W) + p.W) % p.W);
   122	      a += all.cells[CH.S * n + i];
   123	      s += solo.cells[CH.S * n + i];
   124	      cells++;
   125	    }
   126	  return { disc: cells, allSignal: `${a}`, soloSignal: `${s}`, foreignPpm: a > s ? Math.floor((p.PPM * (a - s)) / a) : 0 };
   127	}
   128	
   129	/** A dispersed run is flagged when the measured foreign share of the neighbourhood signal exceeds the frozen threshold; the claim then stays 'dispersed'. */
   130	export const isFlagged = (c: CrossTalk, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): boolean => c.foreignPpm > p.crossTalkMaxPpm;
   131	
   132	export async function calibrationReport(run: JobRunner, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Promise<Calibration> {
   133	  const dev = mergeBlocks(await run(jobsFor(p.devSeeds, ["fixed"], ALL)));
   134	  const noisePatch = p.devSeeds[0];
   135	  const noise = mergeBlocks(await run(jobsFor(p.noiseSeeds, SCHEDULE_IDS, ALL, { patchSeed: noisePatch })));
   136	  const floors: Record<string, Floors> = {};
   137	  for (const sc of SCHEDULE_IDS) floors[sc] = floorsFromNoise(noise, sc);
   138	  // Sensitivity: sense must track the sun better than the no-motility arm by more than the replicate-noise spread of the same readout.
   139	  const err = (b: BlockRecord, st: string, pl: string) => b.runs.fixed[`${st}/${pl}`].readouts.absErr1000 ?? 0;
   140	  const still = dev.flatMap((b) => ["grouped", "dispersed"].map((pl) => err(b, "still", pl)));
   141	  const sense = dev.flatMap((b) => ["grouped", "dispersed"].map((pl) => err(b, "sense", pl)));
   142	  const noiseFloor = Math.max(0, ...["grouped", "dispersed"].map((pl) => spanOver(noise.map((b) => err(b, "still", pl)))));
   143	  const gain2 = medianTimes2(still.map((x, i) => x - sense[i]));
   144	  const sensitivity = { absErrStill1000: still, absErrSense1000: sense, gain2, noiseFloor, senseTracks: gain2 !== null && gain2 > 2 * noiseFloor, floorUsable: floors.fixed.cap <= p.maxFloorCapPpm, sensitive: false, rule: "sensitive = senseTracks and floorUsable. senseTracks: twice the median of (still - sense) absolute circular tracking error over dev blocks and placements must exceed twice the largest replicate-noise spread of the still arm's error. floorUsable: the fixed-schedule capture floor delta_cap must not exceed maxFloorCapPpm" };
   145	  sensitivity.sensitive = sensitivity.senseTracks && sensitivity.floorUsable;
   146	  const crossTalk = { grouped: crossTalkOf("grouped", p, p.devSeeds[0]), dispersed: crossTalkOf("dispersed", p, p.devSeeds[0]), thresholdPpm: p.crossTalkMaxPpm, flagged: false, method: "foreign signal = (signal in a radius-3 disc at body 0 with all bodies) - (signal at the same cells with body 0 alone), at the horizon, fixed schedule, dev seed 1; the signal field has no ownership so this is measured once on the frozen geometry, not per run" };
   147	  crossTalk.flagged = isFlagged(crossTalk.dispersed, p);
   148	  return {
   149	    kind: "movinglight-calibration",
   150	    protocolId: p.id,
   151	    note: "dev and replicate-noise seeds only; no fresh seed is read here",
   152	    schedules: SCHEDULE_IDS.map((sc) => reportOf(sc, meridianPath(sc, p), hashOf(meridianPath(sc, p)), p)),
   153	    genomes: Object.fromEntries(ALL.map((s) => [s, { edit: ARM_EDITS[s], hash: genomeHash(s, p) }])),
   154	    dev: { seeds: p.devSeeds, blocks: dev },
   155	    noise: { seeds: p.noiseSeeds, patchSeed: noisePatch, blocks: noise },
   156	    floors,
   157	    sensitivity,
   158	    crossTalk,
   159	    ladder: { applied: [], note: "workload fits the budget at bench; no reduction step was applied" },
   160	    workload: workload(p),
   161	  };
   162	}

   166	export interface FreshReport {
   167	  seeds: readonly number[];
   168	  blocks: BlockRecord[];
   169	  decisions: Record<string, ScheduleDecision>;
   170	  verdict: Verdict;
   171	  invalidBlocks: number;
   172	}

   174	export async function freshReport(cal: Calibration, run: JobRunner, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Promise<FreshReport> {
   175	  const blocks = mergeBlocks(await run(jobsFor(p.freshSeeds, SCHEDULE_IDS, ALL, { stripSeed: p.freshSeeds[0] })));
   176	  return decideFresh(cal, blocks, p);
   177	}
   178	
   179	export function decideFresh(cal: Pick<Calibration, "floors" | "sensitivity">, blocks: BlockRecord[], p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): FreshReport {
   180	  const decisions: Record<string, ScheduleDecision> = {};
   181	  for (const sc of SCHEDULE_IDS) decisions[sc] = decideSchedule(blocks.map((b) => numbersOf(b, sc)), cal.floors[sc], sc === "fixed", p);
   182	  const invalidBlocks = blocks.filter((b) => b.patchAudit.problems.length > 0).length;
   183	  const verdict = verdictOf({ fixed: decisions.fixed, paused: decisions.paused, reversed: decisions.reversed, wandering: decisions.wandering, floors: cal.floors, insensitive: !cal.sensitivity.sensitive, invalidBlocks, blocks: blocks.length });
   184	  return { seeds: blocks.map((b) => b.seed), blocks, decisions, verdict, invalidBlocks };
   185	}
   186	
   187	export interface FixturesReport {
   188	  syntheticFloor: Record<string, number>;
   189	  coordination: CoordFixture[];
   190	  physical: PhysicalFixture[];
   191	  planted: PlantedReport[];
   192	  plantedFloors: Floors;
   193	  /** The naive metric reads collective motion in these fixtures, and the residual control rejects them. */
   194	  naiveFooled: string[];
   195	}
   196	
   197	export async function fixturesReport(cal: Calibration, run: JobRunner, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Promise<FixturesReport> {
   198	  const floorSynth = { fixed: syntheticFloor("fixed", p), wandering: syntheticFloor("wandering", p) };
   199	  const coord: CoordFixture[] = [];
   200	  for (const kind of ["common-forcing", "coupled-synthetic", "label-takeover", "null-world"] as const) coord.push(coordFixture(kind, "fixed", floorSynth.fixed, p));
   201	  coord.push(coordFixture("common-forcing", "wandering", floorSynth.wandering, p), coordFixture("static-sun", "paused", 0, p));
   202	  const blocks = mergeBlocks(await run(p.fixtureSeeds.map((seed) => ({ seed, schedules: ["fixed"] as ScheduleId[], strategies: ["drift", "sense-nosig", "still"] as StrategyId[] }))));
   203	  const physical = physicalFixtures(cal.floors.fixed, p, blocks);
   204	  const planted = [
   205	    plantedReport("planted-benefit", { fixed: 600_000, paused: 0, reversed: 600_000, wandering: 600_000 }, p),
   206	    plantedReport("planted-benefit-in-paused-too", { fixed: 600_000, paused: 600_000, reversed: 600_000, wandering: 600_000 }, p),
   207	    plantedReport("planted-nothing", { fixed: 0, paused: 0, reversed: 0, wandering: 0 }, p),
   208	  ];
   209	  const naiveFooled = coord.filter((c) => c.naiveReads && c.rejected).map((c) => c.id);
   210	  return { syntheticFloor: floorSynth, coordination: coord, physical, planted, plantedFloors: PLANTED_FLOORS, naiveFooled };
   211	}

   236	export interface Payload {
   237	  kind: "movinglight-evidence";
   238	  version: string;
   239	  protocol: Protocol;
   240	  protocolHash: string;
   241	  registryDigest: string;
   242	  workload: Workload;

   246	  limits: string[];

   249	}

   251	export function sealPayload(body: Omit<Payload, "integritySha256">): Payload {
   252	  return { ...body, integritySha256: hashOf(body) };
   253	}
   254	export function verifySeal(p: Payload): boolean {
   255	  const { integritySha256, ...body } = p;
   256	  return integritySha256 === hashOf(body);
   257	}

   259	export async function buildPayload(registryDigest: string, calibration: Calibration, run: JobRunner, p: Protocol = PROTOCOL): Promise<Payload> {
   260	  const fresh = await freshReport(calibration, run, p);
   261	  const fixtures = await fixturesReport(calibration, run, p);

     2	 * I08 collective sensing under moving light: controlled navigation benchmark, first rung. CPU only (sim-ref), Node 24 (relative imports

    18	  writeFileSync(`${path}.tmp`, text);
    19	  renameSync(`${path}.tmp`, path);

    22	export const REPO = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../", import.meta.url));

    36	let hooked = false;
    37	function registerAliases(): void {
    38	  if (hooked || process.env.VITEST) return;
    39	  hooked = true;
    40	  try {
    41	    registerHooks({
    42	      resolve(specifier, context, next) {
    43	        const m = /^@bl\/(.+)$/.exec(specifier);
    44	        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
    45	      },
    46	    });
    47	  } catch { /* already running under a loader that resolves @bl/* (vitest) */ }
    48	}

    50	const lib = async () => {
    51	  registerAliases();
    52	  const [movinglight, schema] = await Promise.all([import("../../packages/runner/src/movinglight/index.ts"), import("../../packages/schema/src/index.ts")]);
    53	  return { ...movinglight, schema };
    54	};

    56	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
    57	export const sha256 = async (text: string | Uint8Array): Promise<string> => {
    58	  const { schema } = await lib();
    59	  return schema.sha256HexJS(typeof text === "string" ? new TextEncoder().encode(text) : text);
    60	};
    61	export const registryDigest = async (root = REPO): Promise<string> => sha256(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`));

    63	/** Files whose bytes feed the evidence: the library, the simulator and schema it reads, and this tool. */
    64	const SOURCE_DIRS = [SRC_DIR, "packages/schema/src/", "packages/sim-ref/src/"];
    65	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
    66	  const dirs = SOURCE_DIRS.flatMap((d) => readdirSync(`${root}${d}`).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f));
    67	  return [...dirs, "tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts"];
    68	}
    69	
    70	/** At most two child processes; each runs one block job (--job '<json>') and prints the BlockRecord as JSON. Results keep job order. */
    71	export async function pool(specs: any[], workers = 2): Promise<any[]> {
    72	  const results = new Array(specs.length);
    73	  let next = 0;
    74	  const script = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
    75	  const runOne = (spec: any) =>
    76	    new Promise<any>((resolve, reject) => {
    77	      const child = spawn(process.execPath, ["--no-warnings", script, "--job", JSON.stringify(spec)], { stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "inherit"], env: { ...process.env, VITEST: "" } });
    78	      let out = "";
    79	      child.stdout.on("data", (d) => (out += d));
    80	      child.on("error", reject);
    81	      child.on("close", (code) => (code === 0 ? resolve(JSON.parse(out)) : reject(new Error(`job exited ${code}: ${JSON.stringify(spec)}`))));
    82	    });
    83	  const lane = async () => {
    84	    for (;;) {
    85	      const i = next++;
    86	      if (i >= specs.length) return;
    87	      results[i] = await runOne(specs[i]);
    88	    }
    89	  };
    90	  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(workers, 2) }, lane));
    91	  return results;
    92	}
    93	
    94	export async function sourceHashes(root = REPO): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
    95	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    96	  for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) out[f] = await sha256(readFileSync(root + f));
    97	  return out;
    98	}
    99	
   100	export async function makeManifest(payload: any, evidenceText: string, seconds: number): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   101	  const sources = await sourceHashes();
   102	  return {
   103	    kind: "movinglight-evidence-manifest",
   104	    payloadFileSha256: await sha256(evidenceText),
   105	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
   106	    protocolHash: payload.protocolHash,
   107	    registryDigest: payload.registryDigest,
   108	    workload: payload.workload,
   109	    sources,
   110	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", baseCommit: BASE_COMMIT, node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, regenerationSeconds: Math.round(seconds), generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
   111	  };
   112	}

   114	/** --write needs a lock (written before any fresh run) and refuses to overwrite; returns nothing or throws. */
   115	export function writeGuard(paths: { lock: string; evidence: string }, force: boolean): void {
   116	  if (existsSync(paths.evidence) && !force) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   117	  if (!existsSync(paths.lock)) throw new Error("no protocol.lock.json: run --calibrate and --freeze before the fresh run");
   118	}

   120	export const loadCalibration = (path = CALIBRATION): any => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
   121	
   122	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
   123	export async function checkSaved(paths = { evidence: EVIDENCE, manifest: MANIFEST, calibration: CALIBRATION, lock: LOCK }, root = REPO): Promise<string[]> {
   124	  const L = await lib();
   125	  const problems: string[] = [];
   126	  for (const f of Object.values(paths)) if (!existsSync(f)) problems.push(`${f} is missing`);
   127	  if (problems.length) return problems;
   128	  const text = readFileSync(paths.evidence, "utf8");
   129	  const payload = JSON.parse(text);
   130	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.manifest, "utf8"));
   131	  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.lock, "utf8"));
   132	  const calibration = JSON.parse(readFileSync(paths.calibration, "utf8"));
   133	  const canon = L.schema.canonicalJSON;
   134	  if (!L.verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   135	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== (await sha256(text))) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   136	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   137	  if (manifest.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("manifest protocolHash differs from the payload");
   138	  const digest = await registryDigest(root);
   139	  if (payload.registryDigest !== digest) problems.push("seed-registry.json changed since the evidence was written");
   140	  if (lock.protocolHash !== payload.protocolHash) problems.push("protocol.lock.json does not match the payload protocol hash");
   141	  if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("the protocol in code differs from the frozen lock");
   142	  if (canon(payload.protocol) !== canon(L.PROTOCOL)) problems.push("payload protocol differs from the protocol in code");
   143	  if (canon(lock.seeds) !== canon({ dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, noise: L.PROTOCOL.noiseSeeds, fixtures: L.PROTOCOL.fixtureSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds })) problems.push("lock seed lists differ from the protocol");
   144	  if (canon(calibration) !== canon(payload.calibration)) problems.push("calibration.json differs from the calibration section of evidence.json");
   145	  if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(paths.calibration)))) problems.push("lock calibrationSha256 does not match calibration.json");
   146	  for (const sc of L.SCHEDULE_IDS) if (canon(L.PROTOCOL.delta[sc]) !== canon(calibration.floors[sc])) problems.push(`PROTOCOL.delta.${sc} is not the replicate-noise floor in calibration.json`);
   147	  const registry = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${root}experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json`, "utf8")).reservations as { first: number; last: number; owner: string }[];
   148	  const all = [...L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.noiseSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.fixtureSeeds, ...L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds].map((seed, i) => ({ id: `s${i}`, seed }));
   149	  for (const f of L.schema.seedCollisions(all, registry.filter((r) => !r.owner.startsWith(OWN_NAMESPACE)), OWN_NAMESPACE)) problems.push(`seed collision: ${f}`);
   150	  if (!registry.some((r) => r.owner.startsWith(OWN_NAMESPACE) && r.first <= L.PROTOCOL.seedRange.first && r.last >= L.PROTOCOL.seedRange.last)) problems.push("the seed registry has no reservation for this slice");
   151	  // Decisions recomputed from the stored blocks and the stored floors.
   152	  const re = L.decideFresh(payload.calibration, payload.fresh.blocks, L.PROTOCOL);
   153	  if (canon(re.decisions) !== canon(payload.fresh.decisions) || canon(re.verdict) !== canon(payload.fresh.verdict)) problems.push("fresh decisions do not follow the stated decision rules");
   154	  if (L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds.some((s: number, i: number) => payload.fresh.blocks[i]?.seed !== s)) problems.push("fresh blocks are not the frozen fresh seeds in order");
   155	  for (const b of payload.fresh.blocks) for (const e of b.patchAudit.problems) problems.push(`block ${b.seed}: ${e}`);
   156	  // The cheap fixtures recomputed.
   157	  const cal = payload.calibration;
   158	  const cf = L.coordFixture("common-forcing", "fixed", L.syntheticFloor("fixed", L.PROTOCOL), L.PROTOCOL);
   159	  if (canon(cf) !== canon(payload.fixtures.coordination.find((c: any) => c.id === "common-forcing" && c.schedule === "fixed"))) problems.push("common-forcing fixture differs from its recomputation");
   160	  for (const f of payload.fixtures.coordination) if (!f.rejected && !["coupled-synthetic"].includes(f.id)) problems.push(`fixture ${f.id} was not rejected`);
   161	  void cal;
   162	  if (canon(lock.sources ?? null) !== canon(manifest.sources)) problems.push("the source hashes in protocol.lock.json differ from the manifest: the implementation changed between the freeze and the run");
   163	  const manifestSources = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
   164	  if (existsSync(root + SRC_DIR)) for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!manifestSources.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   165	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries<string>(manifest.sources)) {
   166	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   167	    else if ((await sha256(readFileSync(root + file))) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   168	  }
   169	  return problems;
   170	}
   171	
   172	/** Regenerate a frozen two-block subset (first two fresh seeds, all schedules) and the cheap fixtures; require byte equality with the saved payload. */
   173	export async function replayCheck(evidencePath = EVIDENCE, calibrationPath = CALIBRATION, runner?: (specs: any[]) => Promise<any[]>): Promise<string[]> {
   174	  const L = await lib();
   175	  const saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8"));
   176	  const run = runner ?? pool;
   177	  const seeds = L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds.slice(0, 2);
   178	  const specs = L.jobsFor(seeds, L.SCHEDULE_IDS, L.PROTOCOL.strategies, { stripSeed: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds[0] });
   179	  const blocks = L.mergeBlocks(await run(specs));
   180	  const problems: string[] = [];
   181	  blocks.forEach((b: any, i: number) => {
   182	    if (L.schema.canonicalJSON(b) !== L.schema.canonicalJSON(saved.fresh.blocks[i])) problems.push(`regenerated block ${b.seed} differs from evidence.json`);
   183	  });
   184	  const cf = L.coordFixture("common-forcing", "fixed", L.syntheticFloor("fixed", L.PROTOCOL), L.PROTOCOL);
   185	  if (L.schema.canonicalJSON(cf) !== L.schema.canonicalJSON(saved.fixtures.coordination.find((c: any) => c.id === "common-forcing" && c.schedule === "fixed"))) problems.push("regenerated common-forcing fixture differs");
   186	  void calibrationPath;
   187	  return problems;
   188	}
   189	
   190	async function generate(): Promise<any> {
   191	  const L = await lib();
   192	  const cal = loadCalibration();
   193	  return L.buildPayload(await registryDigest(), cal, pool as any);
   194	}
   195	
   196	async function bench(): Promise<void> {
   197	  const L = await lib();
   198	  const t0 = Date.now();
   199	  const b = L.runBlock({ seed: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds[0], schedules: ["fixed"], strategies: ["sense"], placements: ["grouped"] });
   200	  const ms = Date.now() - t0;
   201	  const w = L.workload();
   202	  const perStep = ms / L.PROTOCOL.T;
   203	  console.log(`one world (${b.runs.fixed["sense/grouped"].endHash.slice(0, 8)}): ${L.PROTOCOL.T} steps in ${ms} ms = ${perStep.toFixed(2)} ms/step`);
   204	  console.log(`workload ${w.worldsFresh} fresh + ${w.worldsFixtures} fixture worlds = ${w.steps} steps; projected --write with 2 processes ~ ${Math.round((w.steps * perStep) / 2000)} s (target ${WRITE_TARGET_SECONDS} s)`);
   205	}
   206	
   207	async function main(): Promise<void> {
   208	  const mode = process.argv[2];
   209	  if (mode === "--job") {
   210	    const L = await lib();
   211	    const spec = JSON.parse(process.argv[3]);
   212	    // A fresh seed is never simulated before the protocol lock exists.
   213	    if (L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds.includes(spec.seed) && !existsSync(LOCK)) throw new Error("refusing to run a fresh seed before protocol.lock.json exists");
   214	    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(L.runBlock(spec)));
   215	    return;
   216	  }
   217	  if (mode === "--bench") return bench();
   218	  if (mode === "--calibrate") {
   219	    const L = await lib();
   220	    const t0 = Date.now();
   221	    const cal = await L.calibrationReport(pool as any);
   222	    writeAtomic(CALIBRATION, serialize(cal));
   223	    for (const sc of L.SCHEDULE_IDS) console.log(`floors ${sc}: cap ${cal.floors[sc].cap} ppm, res ${cal.floors[sc].res}`);
   224	    console.log(`sense tracks the sun on dev: ${cal.sensitivity.senseTracks} (2*median gain ${cal.sensitivity.gain2}, 2*noise ${2 * cal.sensitivity.noiseFloor}); capture floor usable: ${cal.sensitivity.floorUsable}; assay sensitive: ${cal.sensitivity.sensitive}`);
   225	    console.log(`cross-talk (dispersed) ${cal.crossTalk.dispersed.foreignPpm} ppm, grouped ${cal.crossTalk.grouped.foreignPpm} ppm, flagged ${cal.crossTalk.flagged}`);
   226	    console.log(`wrote calibration.json (dev and noise seeds only) in ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)} s. Copy the floors into PROTOCOL.delta, then --freeze.`);
   227	    return;
   228	  }
   229	  if (mode === "--freeze") {
   230	    const L = await lib();
   231	    if (!existsSync(CALIBRATION)) throw new Error("run --calibrate first");
   232	    if (existsSync(LOCK) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("protocol.lock.json exists; pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   233	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE)) throw new Error("evidence.json exists: the protocol can no longer be frozen");
   234	    const digest = await registryDigest();
   235	    const cal = loadCalibration();
   236	    for (const sc of L.SCHEDULE_IDS) if (L.schema.canonicalJSON(L.PROTOCOL.delta[sc]) !== L.schema.canonicalJSON(cal.floors[sc])) throw new Error(`PROTOCOL.delta.${sc} must be ${JSON.stringify(cal.floors[sc])} (the replicate-noise floor)`);
   237	    const lock = { kind: "movinglight-protocol-lock", protocolHash: L.protocolHash(digest, L.PROTOCOL), registryDigest: digest, seeds: { dev: L.PROTOCOL.devSeeds, noise: L.PROTOCOL.noiseSeeds, fixtures: L.PROTOCOL.fixtureSeeds, fresh: L.PROTOCOL.freshSeeds }, calibrationSha256: await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION)), sources: await sourceHashes(), note: "written before the fresh-seed run; fresh results never tune the protocol; the source hashes bind the implementation", frozenAt: new Date().toISOString() };
   238	    writeAtomic(LOCK, serialize(lock));
   239	    console.log(`wrote protocol.lock.json ${lock.protocolHash}`);
   240	    return;
   241	  }
   242	  if (mode === "--write") {
   243	    writeGuard({ lock: LOCK, evidence: EVIDENCE }, process.argv.includes("--force"));
   244	    const L = await lib();
   245	    const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(LOCK, "utf8"));
   246	    if (lock.protocolHash !== L.protocolHash(await registryDigest(), L.PROTOCOL)) throw new Error("the protocol in code differs from protocol.lock.json");
   247	    if (lock.calibrationSha256 !== (await sha256(readFileSync(CALIBRATION)))) throw new Error("calibration.json differs from the frozen lock");
   248	    const now = await sourceHashes();
   249	    for (const [f, h] of Object.entries<string>(lock.sources ?? {})) if (now[f] !== h) throw new Error(`${f} changed since the freeze; re-freeze before the fresh run`);
   250	    if (!lock.sources || Object.keys(now).some((f) => !(f in lock.sources))) throw new Error("the lock does not bind every source file; re-freeze");
   251	    // One writer at a time (two pools would exceed the two-process cap).
   252	    const running = `${EVIDENCE}.running`;
   253	    writeFileSync(running, `${process.pid}\n`, { flag: "wx" });
   254	    const t0 = Date.now();
   255	    let payload: any;
   256	    try {
   257	      payload = await generate();
   258	    } finally {
   259	      rmSync(running, { force: true });
   260	    }
   261	    const seconds = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
   262	    const text = serialize(payload);
   263	    const manifestText = serialize(await makeManifest(payload, text, seconds));
   264	    writeAtomic(EVIDENCE, text);
   265	    writeAtomic(MANIFEST, manifestText);
   266	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256} in ${seconds.toFixed(0)} s${seconds > WRITE_TARGET_SECONDS ? ` WARNING: over the ${WRITE_TARGET_SECONDS} s target` : ` (within ${WRITE_TARGET_SECONDS} s)`}; verdict: ${payload.fresh.verdict.label}`);
   267	    return;
   268	  }
   269	  if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   270	    const t0 = Date.now();
   271	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? await checkSaved() : await replayCheck();
   272	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   273	    const seconds = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
   274	    console.log(`${mode} ok${mode === "--replay-check" ? ` (${seconds.toFixed(0)} s${seconds > REPLAY_TARGET_SECONDS ? `, over the ${REPLAY_TARGET_SECONDS} s target` : ""})` : ""}`);
   275	    return;
   276	  }
   277	  console.error("usage: node tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts --bench | --calibrate | --freeze | --write | --check | --replay-check");
   278	  process.exit(2);
   279	}
   280	if (process.argv[1] && pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href === import.meta.url) await main();

    43	    const blocks = seeds.map((seed) => runBlock({ seed, schedules: ["fixed"], strategies: ["drift", "sense-nosig", "still"] }, SHORT));
    44	    const fx = physicalFixtures({ cap: 20_000, res: 1_000_000_000 }, SHORT, blocks);
    45	    expect(fx.map((f) => f.id)).toEqual(["blind-drifters-physical", "crowding-physical", "stationary-growth"]);
    46	    for (const f of fx) {
    47	      expect(f.B.every((b) => b === 0)).toBe(true);
    48	      expect(typeof f.residualWithinFloor).toBe("boolean");
    49	    }
    50	    // The residual floor is huge here, so stationary-growth's residual is within it and every fixture is rejected.
    51	    for (const f of fx) expect(f.rejected).toBe(true);
    52	    // A tiny residual floor flips stationary-growth only: it is rejected only when its residual stays inside the floor.
    53	    const strict = physicalFixtures({ cap: 20_000, res: -1_000_000_000_000 }, SHORT, blocks);
    54	    expect(strict.find((f) => f.id === "stationary-growth")!.rejected).toBe(false);
    55	    expect(strict.find((f) => f.id === "crowding-physical")!.rejected).toBe(true);
    56	  }, 120_000);
    57	});
     1	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     2	import { coordination, type Track } from "../src/movinglight/coordination.ts";
     3	import { SYNTH, syntheticTracks } from "../src/movinglight/fixtures.ts";

     6	
     7	const path = meridianPath("wandering");
     8	const cov = (kind: Parameters<typeof syntheticTracks>[0], pl: "grouped" | "dispersed", seed: number) => coordination(syntheticTracks(kind, pl, seed, "wandering"), path);
     9	
    10	describe("movinglight coordination", () => {
    11	  it("common forcing raises naive alignment in group and dispersed set equally and gives covariance ~0", () => {
    12	    let maxDiff = 0, gsum = 0, dsum = 0, ng = 0;
    13	    for (const s of P.noiseSeeds) {
    14	      const g = cov("common-forcing", "grouped", s), d = cov("common-forcing", "dispersed", s);
    15	      expect(g.signAgreementPpm).toBeGreaterThan(800_000);
    16	      expect(d.signAgreementPpm).toBeGreaterThan(800_000);
    17	      gsum += g.signAgreementPpm!;
    18	      dsum += d.signAgreementPpm!;
    19	      ng++;
    20	      maxDiff = Math.max(maxDiff, Math.abs(g.covMilliQ2! - d.covMilliQ2!));
    21	    }
    22	    expect(Math.abs(gsum - dsum) / ng).toBeLessThan(100_000);
    23	    const coupled = cov("coupled-synthetic", "grouped", P.noiseSeeds[0]).covMilliQ2! - cov("coupled-synthetic", "dispersed", P.noiseSeeds[0]).covMilliQ2!;
    24	    expect(coupled).toBeGreaterThan(5 * maxDiff);
    25	  });
    26	  it("coupled agents give a residual above the floor", () => {
    27	    const quiet = { ...SYNTH, couplingQ: 0 };
    28	    const withC = coordination(syntheticTracks("coupled-synthetic", "grouped", 5, "wandering"), path).covMilliQ2!;
    29	    const without = coordination(syntheticTracks("coupled-synthetic", "grouped", 5, "wandering", P, quiet), path).covMilliQ2!;
    30	    expect(withC).toBeGreaterThan(without);
    31	  });
    32	  it("static sun gives unavailable not zero", () => {
    33	    const tr = syntheticTracks("static-sun", "grouped", 3, "paused");
    34	    expect(coordination(tr, meridianPath("paused")).covMilliQ2).toBeNull();
    35	  });
    36	  it("zero-displacement and missing-lineage pairs are handled and pair counts reported", () => {
    37	    const WQ = P.W * P.Q;
    38	    const t = [32, 64, 96, 128];
    39	    const tr: Track = { t, q: [[0, 100, 200, 300], [0, 90, 220, 280], [null, null, 50, 60], [0, null, 10, 10]] };
    40	    const c = coordination(tr, meridianPath("paused"));
    41	    expect(c.intervals).toBe(3);
    42	    expect(c.pairs).toBe(1);
    43	    expect(c.pairIntervals).toBe(3);
    44	    const frozen: Track = { t, q: [[5, 5, 5, 5], [9, 9, 9, 9]] };
    45	    expect(coordination(frozen, meridianPath("paused")).covMilliQ2).toBeNull();
    46	    const jump: Track = { t, q: [[0, 100, 200, 300], [0, 90, 200 + 10 * P.Q, 280]] };
    47	    expect(coordination(jump, meridianPath("paused")).excluded).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    48	    void WQ;
    49	  });

     1	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     2	import { bootstrapLowerBound2, decideSchedule, medianTimes2, signP, verdictOf, type BlockNumbers } from "../src/movinglight/decision.ts";
     3	import { PLANTED_FLOORS, plantedTable } from "../src/movinglight/fixtures.ts";
     4	
     5	const F = PLANTED_FLOORS;
     6	const FS = { fixed: F, paused: F, reversed: F, wandering: F };
     7	const arm = (g: number, d: number, covG = 0, covD = 0, sg = 600_000, sd = 600_000) => ({ grouped: { capturePpm: g, survivalPpm: sg, cov: covG }, dispersed: { capturePpm: d, survivalPpm: sd, cov: covD } });
     8	const block = (o: Partial<Record<string, ReturnType<typeof arm>>> = {}): BlockNumbers => ({ sense: arm(300_000, 300_000), "sense-nosig": arm(300_000, 300_000), "sense-nomot": arm(300_000, 300_000), drift: arm(300_000, 300_000), still: arm(300_000, 300_000), ...o });
     9	const eight = (f: (i: number) => BlockNumbers) => Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => f(i));
    10	const benefit = (i: number) => block({ sense: arm(700_000 + i, 300_000, 9000, 0) });
    11	
    12	describe("movinglight decision", () => {
    13	  it("needs raw S, B, sign test, delta floor, bootstrap bound, residual, survival and B_drift all together", () => {
    14	    const ok = decideSchedule(eight(benefit), F, true);
    15	    expect(ok.criteria.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(["i", "ii", "iii", "iv", "v", "vi"]);
    16	    expect(ok.criteria.every((c) => c.pass)).toBe(true);
    17	    const noResidual = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(700_000 + i, 300_000, 0, 0) })), F, true);
    18	    expect(noResidual.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "iv")!.pass).toBe(false);
    19	    const drifterEqual = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(700_000 + i, 300_000, 9000, 0), drift: arm(700_000 + i, 300_000) })), F, true);
    20	    expect(drifterEqual.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "vi")!.pass).toBe(false);
    21	    const worseSurvival = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(700_000 + i, 300_000, 9000, 0, 100_000, 900_000) })), F, true);
    22	    expect(worseSurvival.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "v")!.pass).toBe(false);
    23	    const withinFloor = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(310_000 + i, 300_000, 9000, 0) })), F, true);
    24	    expect(withinFloor.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "ii")!.pass).toBe(false);
    25	  });
    26	  it("positive B with negative S is not supported", () => {
    27	    // S = -50k, N = -200k: B = +150k, but grouped sensing is worse than dispersed sensing.
    28	    const d = decideSchedule(eight(() => block({ sense: arm(250_000, 300_000, 9000, 0), "sense-nosig": arm(100_000, 300_000) })), F, true);
    29	    expect(d.B.positives).toBe(8);
    30	    expect(d.S.positives).toBe(0);
    31	    expect(d.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "i")!.pass).toBe(false);
    32	    expect(d.nonPositive).toBe(true);
    33	  });
    34	  it("7 of 8 gives p = 9/256", () => {
    35	    expect(signP(7, 8)).toBe("9/256");
    36	    expect(signP(8, 8)).toBe("1/256");
    37	    expect(9 / 256).toBeLessThan(1 / 20);
    38	    expect(signP(6, 8)).toBe("37/256");
    39	  });
    40	  it("zeros count as not positive", () => {
    41	    const d = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(i < 6 ? 700_000 : 300_000, 300_000, 9000, 0) })), F, true);
    42	    expect(d.S.positives).toBe(6);
    43	    expect(d.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "i")!.pass).toBe(false);
    44	  });
    45	  it("extinct arms keep terminal outcomes and blocks are not dropped", () => {
    46	    // An extinct dispersed sense arm has capture and survival of its own terminal outcome (here zero), and still counts as a block.
    47	    const blocks = eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(700_000, i === 0 ? 0 : 300_000, 9000, 0, 600_000, 0) }));
    48	    const d = decideSchedule(blocks, F, true);
    49	    expect(d.complete).toBe(8);
    50	    expect(d.S.of).toBe(8);
    51	    const missing = decideSchedule([...blocks.slice(0, 7), {}], F, true);
    52	    expect(missing.complete).toBe(7);
    53	    expect(missing.criteria.find((c) => c.id === "i")!.pass).toBe(false);
    54	    // Seven uniformly passing blocks are not eight: the frozen block count is the denominator.
    55	    const seven = decideSchedule(eight(benefit).slice(0, 7), F, true);
    56	    expect(seven.blocks).toBe(8);
    57	    expect(seven.complete).toBe(7);
    58	    expect(seven.criteria.every((c) => c.pass)).toBe(false);
    59	  });
    60	  it("paused control with a benefit gives inconclusive-confounded", () => {
    61	    const t = plantedTable({ fixed: 600_000, paused: 600_000, reversed: 600_000, wandering: 600_000 }, 1);
    62	    const d = (s: string, full: boolean) => decideSchedule(t[s], F, full);
    63	    const v = verdictOf({ fixed: d("fixed", true), paused: d("paused", false), reversed: d("reversed", false), wandering: d("wandering", false), floors: FS, insensitive: false, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 });
    64	    expect(v.label).toBe("inconclusive-confounded");
    65	  });
    66	  it("floors come from noise seeds only", () => {
    67	    // The tool's floors are computed by floorsFromNoise from the replicate-noise blocks only; the decision takes them as an argument.
    68	    expect(decideSchedule.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    69	    expect(medianTimes2([1, 5, 3, 7])).toBe(8);
    70	    expect(bootstrapLowerBound2([5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5], 200, 1, 100_000)).toBe(10);
    71	    expect(bootstrapLowerBound2([1, 2, 3], 200, 7, 100_000)).toBe(bootstrapLowerBound2([1, 2, 3], 200, 7, 100_000));
    72	  });
    73	  it("ties at zero with a positive majority are inconclusive, negative comparators disagreeing are confounded", () => {
    74	    // S and B tie at zero in the median but five of eight blocks are positive: neither pass nor fail.
    75	    const tie = decideSchedule(eight((i) => block({ sense: arm(300_000 + (i < 5 ? 1 : i < 6 ? 0 : -1), 300_000, 9000, 0) })), F, true);
    76	    expect(tie.nonPositive).toBe(false);
    77	    const v = verdictOf({ fixed: tie, paused: tie, reversed: tie, wandering: tie, floors: FS, insensitive: false, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 });
    78	    expect(v.label).toBe("inconclusive");
    79	    // Negative B (sense worse than its silent twin) but positive B_drift (better than the drifter): the comparators disagree.
    80	    const dis = decideSchedule(eight(() => block({ sense: arm(300_000, 300_000), "sense-nosig": arm(600_000, 300_000), drift: arm(0, 300_000) })), F, true);
    81	    expect(dis.B.median2!).toBeLessThan(0);
    82	    const vd = verdictOf({ fixed: dis, paused: dis, reversed: dis, wandering: dis, floors: FS, insensitive: false, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 });
    83	    expect(vd.label).toBe("inconclusive-confounded");
    84	  });
    85	  it("paused control is judged against its own floor", () => {
    86	    const small = { cap: 100_000, res: 2_000 };
    87	    const t = plantedTable({ fixed: 600_000, paused: 150_000, reversed: 600_000, wandering: 600_000 }, 2);
    88	    const d = (s: string, full: boolean, f: typeof F) => decideSchedule(t[s], f, full);
    89	    const mk = (pausedFloor: typeof F) => verdictOf({ fixed: d("fixed", true, F), paused: d("paused", false, pausedFloor), reversed: d("reversed", false, F), wandering: d("wandering", false, F), floors: { ...FS, paused: pausedFloor }, insensitive: false, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 }).label;
    90	    expect(mk(small)).toBe("inconclusive-confounded");
    91	    expect(mk({ cap: 1_000_000, res: 2_000 })).toBe("supported");
    92	  });
    93	  it("insensitive assay is unsupported-measurement, not no benefit", () => {
    94	    const d = decideSchedule(eight(() => block()), F, true);
    95	    const v = verdictOf({ fixed: d, paused: d, reversed: d, wandering: d, floors: FS, insensitive: true, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 });
    96	    expect(v.label).toBe("unsupported-measurement");
    97	    expect(verdictOf({ fixed: d, paused: d, reversed: d, wandering: d, floors: FS, insensitive: false, invalidBlocks: 0, blocks: 8 }).label).toBe("unsupported-within-tested-domain");
    98	  });
    99	});

     7	describe("movinglight schedule", () => {
     8	  it("meridian equals RefSim.light argmax for fixed, paused, wandering", () => {
     9	    for (const sc of ["fixed", "paused", "wandering"] as const) {
    10	      const sim = new RefSim(allocState(worldConfig(1, sc)));
    11	      const path = meridianPath(sc);
    12	      for (let t = 0; t <= PROTOCOL.T; t += 7) {
    13	        let best = -1, arg = -1;
    14	        for (let x = 0; x < PROTOCOL.W; x++) {
    15	          const L = sim.light(x, 0, t);
    16	          if (L > best) { best = L; arg = x; }
    17	        }
    18	        expect(arg).toBe(path[t]);
    19	      }
    20	    }
    21	  });
    22	  it("realised integer path frozen, first-move, turn step and realised speed ratio reported", () => {
    23	    const fixed = reportOf("fixed", meridianPath("fixed"), "h");

    29	    expect(paused.firstMoveStep).toBeNull();
    30	    expect(paused.meanSpeedPer1000).toBe(0);
    31	    expect(meridianPath("fixed")).toEqual(meridianPath("fixed"));
    32	  });
    33	  it("reversed schedule has eastward and westward legs of at least k steps", () => {

    38	  });

    41	    const turn = reportOf("reversed", path, "h").turnStep!;
    42	    const from = scoredFrom("reversed", path);

  light(x: number, y: number, step: number): number {
    const c = this.cfg;
    const lx = x % c.tileW;
    const ly = y % c.tileH;
    let L = c.lightBase;
    const mode = lightModeId(c.lightMode);
    if (mode === 0) L += c.lightAmp;
    else if (mode === 1) L += divu(mulu(c.lightAmp, ly), c.tileH - 1);
    else if (mode === 2) {
      if ((((lx >> 5) + (ly >> 5)) & 1) === 0) L += c.lightAmp;
    } else {
      // Rotating planet: a tent of light centred on the sun's meridian.
      const P = c.dayPeriod ?? 0;
      let sun = P > 0 ? divu(mulu(step % P, c.tileW), P) : 0;
      const WP = c.wanderPeriod ?? 0;
      if (WP > 0) {
        const ph = divu(mulu(step % WP, 512), WP);
        const tri = 256 - Math.abs(ph - 256);
        sun = (sun + (((c.wanderAmp ?? 0) * tri) >> 8)) % c.tileW;
      }
      const half = c.tileW >> 1;
      const d0 = (lx + c.tileW - sun) % c.tileW;
      const d = Math.min(d0, c.tileW - d0);
      L += divu(mulu(c.lightAmp, half - d), half);
    }
    if (c.seasonPeriod > 0) {
      const ph = divu(mulu(step % c.seasonPeriod, 512), c.seasonPeriod);
      const tri = Math.abs(ph - 256);
      L += (c.seasonAmp * tri) >> 8;
    }
    return clampi(L, 0, 255);
  }

  step(): StepResult {
    if (this.state.step >= MAX_STEP) throw new Error(`step limit ${MAX_STEP} reached`);
    this.affinity();
    this.flow();
    this.transport();
    const r = this.react();
    this.state.step += 1;
    if ([this.state.lightIn, this.state.heatOut, ...this.state.flux].some((v) => v >= LEDGER_MAX))
      throw new Error("ledger total reached 2^63; the run cannot continue exactly");
    return r;
  }

// port, which would make /api proxy to itself. bin/dev sets COORDINATOR_PORT.
const coordinatorUrl = `http://localhost:${process.env.COORDINATOR_PORT ?? "4000"}`;

/**
 * A request's path, decoded and normalised (`.` and `..` segments resolved, repeated slashes folded, never above the
 * root), its raw form, and its query; null when the path does not decode. Every check below reads the normalised path,
 * so `//404.html`, `/./404.html` or `/x/../404.html` are the not-found page by name too, and no existence check looks
 * outside the root.
 */
function splitUrl(url = "/"): { pathname: string; decoded: string; path: string; search: string } | null {
  const q = url.indexOf("?");
  const [pathname, search] = q < 0 ? [url, ""] : [url.slice(0, q), url.slice(q)];
  try {
    const decoded = decodeURIComponent(pathname);
    return { pathname, decoded, path: posix.normalize(posix.join("/", decoded)), search };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * The not-found page asked for by name (`/404.html`, `/404.html/`, `/404%2ehtml`, `/404.HTML`): it answers 404 too, as in
 * Caddy, whose path matcher ignores case. Case is ignored here as well, since a case-insensitive disk (macOS) would
 * otherwise serve `/404.HTML` with 200.
 */
const namesNotFound = (path: string) => path.replace(/\/+$/, "").toLowerCase() === "/404.html";

/** The queries Vite's module loaders read (`?raw`, `?url`, `?import`, `?html-proxy` and the like). */
const VITE_MODULE_QUERY = /[?&](?:import|raw|url|inline|html-proxy|worker|sharedworker|direct)(?:[=&]|$)/;
/** Fetch destinations Vite's transform middleware never answers (vite 8, middlewares/transform.ts). */
const DOCUMENT_DESTS = new Set(["document", "iframe", "frame", "fencedframe"]);

/**
 * A module request for an .html file, which Vite's transform middleware answers as JavaScript: a module query, from a
 * fetch that is not a document's, that Vite would transform (a script fetch, an `import` query or an `html-proxy` one,
 * the same tests its middleware makes). Anything else asking for the page by name is a navigation or a plain fetch,
 * which a static server would answer with the file and 200, so it gets the page.
 */
function viteModuleRequest(req: Connect.IncomingMessage, search: string): boolean {
  const dest = req.headers["sec-fetch-dest"];
  if (typeof dest === "string" && DOCUMENT_DESTS.has(dest)) return false;
  if (!VITE_MODULE_QUERY.test(search)) return false;
  return dest === "script" || /[?&]import=?(?:&|$)/.test(search) || /[?&]html-proxy\b/.test(search);
}

/**
 * An unknown page answers 404 with the site's own page, 404.html, as deploy/Caddyfile does in production. Runs after
 * Vite's mpa html fallback, which has already rewritten a known page to its .html file. Only requests Vite's fallback
 * would treat as a page (GET or HEAD, with an Accept that names text/html or *\/* or is absent) get the page; a fetch
 * asking for JSON keeps Vite's empty 404. A directory with an index.html asked for without its slash answers 308 to
 * the slash, keeping the query, which is what Caddy's `redir` lines do for the eight top-level pages. Known differences
 * from Caddy: dev sends any such directory to its slash (`/garden`, which Caddy serves at 200 without one, and dev-only
 * directories such as `/dist`); Vite also answers `/<name>` with `/<name>.html` when that file exists (`/selftest`);
 * dev has no `/<page>/world/<id>/` copies; a path that does not decode (`/%zz`) gets the page here, where Caddy
 * answers its own empty 400; and on a case-blind disk (macOS) a page asked for in another case is found (`/ABOUT/`
 * answers 200 in dev and preview, `/Lab/index.HTML` 200 in dev), where production's Linux disk answers 404, and `/About`
 * is sent to `/About/` where Caddy's case-blind `redir` sends it to `/about/`. Only the not-found page's own name is
 * matched without case here.
 */
function notFound(root: string, page: () => Promise<string>): Connect.NextHandleFunction {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    const url = splitUrl(req.url);
    const named = url !== null && namesNotFound(url.path);
    const accept = req.headers.accept;
    const asPage = (req.method === "GET" || req.method === "HEAD") && (accept === undefined || accept === "" || accept.includes("text/html") || accept.includes("*/*"));
    if (!named && !asPage) return next();
    if (url && !named && url.path.endsWith(".html") && existsSync(join(root, url.path))) return next();
    // Only a path that is already in normal form is redirected, to itself plus a slash: `//name` (another host) or a
    // path with dot segments gets the page instead.
    if (url && !named && url.decoded === url.path && !url.path.endsWith("/") && existsSync(join(root, url.path, "index.html"))) {
      res.writeHead(308, { Location: `${url.pathname}/${url.search}` }).end();
      return;
    }
    page().then((html) => {
      res.writeHead(404, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
      res.end(req.method === "HEAD" ? undefined : html);
    }, next);
  };
}

/**
 * The static servers would answer the not-found page asked for by name with 200, so those requests are taken first. In
 * dev, a module request for it (`import text from "/404.html?raw"`, which Vite sends as `?raw&import`) still goes on
 * to Vite's transforms, which answer it as JavaScript; a navigation or plain fetch with the same query does not, since
 * Vite would not transform it and its static files would serve `/404.HTML?raw` with 200 on a case-blind disk.
 * `vite preview` has no transforms, so there every request for it is taken.
 */
const namedNotFound = (handle: Connect.NextHandleFunction, { modules }: { modules: boolean }): Connect.NextHandleFunction => (req, res, next) => {
  const url = splitUrl(req.url);
  if (!url || !namesNotFound(url.path)) return next();
  return modules && viteModuleRequest(req, url.search) ? next() : handle(req, res, next);
};

codex
- **[P2] Missing paused controls can still yield `supported`.** [decision.ts:196](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/decision.ts:196) checks completeness only for fixed; line 205 accepts paused by its remaining median. Replacing seven paused blocks with `{}` in the planted table still returns `supported`. **Fix:** enforce completeness across every required schedule before assigning a benefit label.

- **[P2] Placements change illumination as well as proximity.** [arms.ts:110](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/arms.ts:110) preserves mean offset, not PLAN’s offset distribution. Grouped x positions are 17/23; dispersed positions are 14/2. With dev seed 8700001, initial biomass-weighted light is **162.29 versus 127.31**. Strategy-dependent responses to this lighting difference can enter B without collective benefit. **Fix:** preserve each body’s sun-relative x position while changing spacing, or add a matched illumination control.

- **[P2] Common-forcing rejection is forced by identical trajectories.** [fixtures.ts:38](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/fixtures.ts:38) gives grouped and dispersed agents identical noise increments; placement changes only constant offsets. Their displacement statistics therefore cancel identically. Removing sun subtraction in an in-memory probe still produced eight zero residuals for both schedules. **Fix:** use independent noise streams between placements, calibrate their null floor, and add assertions that fail when sun subtraction is removed.

- **[P2] The positive “coupled” fixture has no neighbour interaction.** [fixtures.ts:39](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/fixtures.ts:39) adds the same external random fluctuation to every grouped agent. No agent reads another agent’s state, yet the fixture reports `coordination-detected`. **Fix:** implement the planned neighbour-dependent update and verify that perturbing one neighbour affects others while zero coupling rejects.

- **[P2] Stationary-growth accepts arbitrarily negative residuals as within floor.** [fixtures.ts:161](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/fixtures.ts:161) counts only residuals above the positive floor. Residuals of −1,000,000,000 against floor 45,205,021 still produce `residualWithinFloor: true` and `rejected: true`. Missing residuals can also pass. **Fix:** require at least six available residuals satisfying `Math.abs(r) <= floor`, matching criterion (iv).

- **[P2] Artifact checks accept failed fixtures.** [movinglight.ts:156](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/tools/movinglight/movinglight.ts:156) recomputes only common-forcing/fixed and never checks physical or planted outcomes. Changing a physical fixture to `rejected: false`, then updating the ordinary seals, still makes `checkSaved()` return `[]`. Replay also checks only that one synthetic fixture. **Fix:** validate the complete fixture inventory and expected outcomes; recompute all cheap fixtures and retain enough physical data to verify those decisions.

- **[P2] “Foreign signal fraction” is not identified by this probe.** [evidence.ts:107](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/packages/runner/src/movinglight/evidence.ts:107) compares interacting, moving `sense` worlds against a solo world. Competition, movement and emission change the focal body’s own contribution, so `(all − solo)/all` does not isolate foreign signal. Consequently, the reported 958,904 ppm cannot establish that 95.9% is foreign. **Fix:** use the planned controlled emitter probe, or label this a counterfactual signal-difference proxy and withdraw the ownership percentage.

- **[P2] The reservation breaks existing I06 verification.** [seed-registry.json:20](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/experiments/discovery/seed-registry.json:20) changes I06’s globally bound registry digest. I06’s `--check` now fails; EVIDENCE.md acknowledges four failing tests. **Fix:** introduce backward-compatible registry snapshot or reservation-scoped verification while retaining current global collision checks, and resolve the regression before acceptance.

- **[P3] Page panels misreport medians.** [render.ts:61](/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i08-moving-light/apps/lab/src/moving-light/render.ts:61) returns the lower middle value for eight blocks. Fixed/grouped `sense` displays 1,282,713 ppm; the actual median is 1,554,716 ppm. **Fix:** average the two middle values, consistently with the decision code.

Read-only verification: I08 `--check` passes; its two-block `--replay-check` passes in 65 seconds. No future-position lag lookup or physics/schema/golden changes found. Deployment exclusions mirror `/niche/`. Soup differences come from newer `origin/main`, not the freeze commit. Push-before-record-run is supported locally; execution of the earlier fresh-seed tests remains unproven.

**Verdict: hold—resolve the P2 decision, fixture, measurement and integration defects before acceptance.**
